- [t24 step-2 wave, row-1257 sweep, 2026-07-24] **`tranche3_negative_probes.rs`'s collision_lookup probes were comparing a 36-byte section against a 424-byte reference slice — a VACUOUS `assert_ne!` (fixed), and a residual 2-byte "genuine" mismatch (OPEN).** The t24 children wave slid every region from `load_object` down by −6, so the wave's row-1257 sweep enumerated every hardcoded engine address in `crates/`. Outcomes: `children_port` doc comments UPDATED; `repin_pins.rs` SOUND_API baselines UPDATED (the wave's hand-edit); `load_object_port.rs:228/234` doc comments were STALE FROM AN OLDER ERA (`0x3FDC`/`0x4BA6` — neither the pre- nor post-wave geometry) → refreshed; `collision_lookup_port.rs:353/394` assertion MESSAGES likewise stale → de-addressed. **The real catch:** `tranche3_negative_probes.rs` hardcoded `collision_lookup` at `0x4A76` with the reference window `&refrom[0x4A76..0x4C1E]` — a 0x1A8-byte slice compared against the region's 0x24 bytes, so BOTH `assert_ne!` probes (doctored `CTYPE_AIR`, moved `Tile_Cache_GetCollision`) were trivially true regardless of the doctoring. MIGRATED to pins (`pins::COLLISION_LOOKUP.plain_base/plain_len` + a `collision_ref_window()` helper + `genuine_tile_cache_vma()` from `pins::TILE_CACHE_GET_COLLISION`), so a re-pin can never re-stale them; all 8 probes stay green. **ROOT-CAUSED AND EXTINGUISHED same day (overseer ruling R3 — a fragility class dies on its SECOND bite, and t23's row-1257 migration was the first).** The residual 2-byte mismatch at offset `0x5` (`0x34` vs `0x36`) was NOT the local twin: it was `cache_ram_labels()` phasing the four `Cache_*` RAM labels at a hardcoded `$FFFFA834` while the real plain `Cache_Left_Col` is `$FFFFA836` (`pins::CACHE_LEFT_COL.plain`) — the reference's first `cmp.w (Cache_Left_Col).w, d0` encodes `A836`, the probe encoded `A834`. The SAME stale-hardcoded-fixture class ran through the whole file: `vdp_init` placed at `0x1C14`/`0x48` (real `0x1C08`/`0x4A`), its window `[0x1C14..0x1C5C]`, `BootData_VDPRegs` at `$3CE` (real `$3C2`), `Tile_Cache_GetCollision` at `$418E` (real `$43D8`), `VDP_Shadow_Table`'s inter-label pad hardcoded at 19. ALL migrated to pins (`CACHE_LEFT_COL`, `VDP_INIT.plain_base/len`, `BOOT_DATA_VDP_REGS`, `TILE_CACHE_GET_COLLISION`, `VDP_SHADOW_TABLE`/`VDP_DIRTY_MASK`), plus **TWO NEW POSITIVE CONTROLS** — `collision_lookup_undoctored_compile_equals_the_reference_window` and `vdp_init_undoctored_compile_equals_the_reference_window` — which turn the file's prose claim ("FALSIFIED (restore-real-value): the same compile path equals the reference window") into an executable gate: a fixture that drifts from the pins now fails LOUD instead of silently de-genuining every `assert_ne!` beside it. Verified by falsification: with the doctoring removed, `collision_lookup_doctored_ctype_air_twin_produces_different_bytes` and `collision_lookup_wrong_tile_cache_vma_changes_the_bra_bytes` BOTH FAIL (they passed before the fix) — the probes now bite for the right reason. 10/10 green undoctored. **THE GENERAL LESSON (the reason this was invisible to every gate): a negative probe needs a POSITIVE CONTROL in the same file, or its fixtures can rot without any test noticing.** — CLOSED (class extinguished; positive-control pattern is the standing prescription for `assert_ne!`-style probes).

- [t24 pass-1 step 4, 2026-07-24] **DEMANDED FEATURE SHIPPED — struct-field displacement over a SPLICED base register inside a comptime-fn template (`Sst.field({sst})`).** The step-4 construct pass tried to build `refresh_piece_count` (the 12-instruction duplicate shared by animate.emp and children.emp) and the template would not lower: `peek_inner_reg` (`eval/asm.rs`) resolved the base register ONLY from a literal one-segment path, so BOTH field-displacement forms — the bare D6.A3 `f(aN)` and the qualified D6.A4 `S.f(aN)` — fell through to comptime eval and died as `unknown name \`Sst.mappings\``. This is the exact sibling of the spliced INDEX-register gap (row 1036, built 2026-07-14 as `map_an_indexed`'s eval path): the same asymmetry, one slot over. FIX: `peek_inner_reg` now also accepts a one-segment path BOUND to a `Value::Reg` in the template env — a binding read, never an eval, so the peek stays silent and its callers keep owning the diagnostics. TDD: `tranche24_spelling_probes::struct_field_displacement_over_spliced_base_matches_as` (a 2-field struct touched through a spliced base, byte-diffed against the literal-register twin spelling). CONSEQUENCE: multi-instruction templates over TYPED records are now expressible at all — that class was silently closed before, which is why every corpus template to date either takes no struct or spells numeric displacements. — CLOSED (shipped in-tranche).

- [t24 pass-1 step 3(a), 2026-07-24] **Named stack slots — the hand-computed-displacement class has no language answer.** `CreateChild_Linked` keeps its running spawn position in two stack longs and reads them as `4(sp)` / `(sp)`, with a comment ("Stack layout: [running_Y][running_X] at sp+0 and sp+4") that exists only because the language cannot name the slots. Every such site is gate-blind by construction (the byte gate proves the .emp and the twin agree on a WRONG offset just as happily as a right one) and it is exactly the lens-C2 hazard class. ASK: a `stack_frame { name: type, … }` construct (or a `let` binding over `-(sp)` pushes) that names slots and computes displacements at comptime, so a pushed value is read by NAME and a mis-computed offset becomes unrepresentable. Demand so far: 1 file, 4 sites (2 reads + 2 in-place adds), plus the `addq #8, sp` release whose size is likewise hand-derived. — RECORDED (ask; no build — the construct is grammar-sized, per step-4 verb (c)).

- [t24 pass-1 step 3(a), 2026-07-24] **Noticing-clause proposal: a "does this repeated shape DO anything?" pre-filter for the ceremony scan.** children.asm's most repeated non-trivial shape was the alloc-fail descriptor SKIP-WALK — five copies over two record strides, ~25 lines, a textbook `emit_piece_loop`-class template candidate. It is also entirely DEAD WORK (it advances a1, which is a declared clobber that no caller reads), so the right answer was to DELETE all five, not to build a template that emits the duplication more elegantly. The ceremony scan as written ("where does the file spend many lines saying something simple?") would have proposed the construct. PROPOSED LINE: before proposing a construct for a repeated shape, ask whether the shape has an OBSERVABLE EFFECT — a construct that mechanizes dead work makes the dead work permanent and harder to see. — RECORDED (proposed step-3(a) interrogation line; ratification is the gate's call).

- [t24 pass-1 step 3(b), 2026-07-24] **`ObjRoutine` NAME COLLISION — two different types, both in scope in object modules (overseer ruling R4: ledger, no in-tranche rename).** `engine/system/types.emp:37` declares `pub newtype ObjRoutine = u16` (the SST `code_addr` domain — an offset from ObjCodeBase), while `engine/objects/core.emp:21` declares `pub type ObjRoutine = proc (a0: *Sst) preserves(a0, d7)` (the dispatch signature used by `jsr (a1) as ObjRoutine`). Object modules can have both names in scope; nothing today disambiguates them beyond position. RECOMMENDED RESOLUTION (overseer's, recorded for the corpus-wide rename batch): **core.emp's proc type takes the new name** (`ObjCode` / `ObjProc`) — it is the younger, narrower concept, while the `types.emp` newtype is the one that spreads through typed signatures (t24 adopted it at two proc params). Rename is corpus-wide and byte-neutral. — RECORDED (at-next-touch of core.emp, or its own micro-batch).

- [t24 pass-1 step 5, 2026-07-24] **THE $8000 BANK-SHIFT CLASS: a DEBUG-ONLY engine growth can slide the GAME OBJECT BANK, and four families of harness fixture silently assume it cannot.** Measured, not theorized. The first cut of the chain-contract asserts put one on each of the four chaining creators (+0x14E debug bytes). That pushed engine symbols across **$8000**, so the AS width rule widened the PLAYER code's `jsr (Sym).w` call sites (inside the object bank at `org $10000`) to `abs.l`, +2 bytes each — sliding the whole DEBUG object bank **+0xC**. Everything keyed to "the two banks coincide" broke at once: (1) `objdef_port` fed `pins::TEST_*.plain` to BOTH shape arms; (2) `test_objects_port` hardcoded `TEST_SOLID/TEST_PARTICLE.plain_base` for both; (3) six `mixed_dac_rom` debug arms placed act_descriptor/sonic_anims/particle_anims/test_solid/test_particle at hardcoded or plain-only bases (overlap + link errors); (4) the AS-side gate resume orgs in `games/sonic4/main.asm` (whose comment explicitly claimed the bank is "SHAPE-INVARIANT — the bank's contents up to here don't change with __DEBUG__") and `act_descriptor.asm`. ALL FOUR FIXED: (1)+(2)+(3) are now pins-derived per shape (permanent hardening — they stay correct whether or not the banks coincide), and (4)'s comment now states the conditional truth and names the trigger. THE WAVE ITSELF was then trimmed to two asserts (the two sites that can actually fire — Complex/FlipAware have zero call sites, so an assert there can never fire but its message blob still costs debug bytes), which put the bank back at parity: debug growth +0x46. **LESSON for every future debug-shape addition: `assert` blobs are not free at the SYSTEM level — past a threshold they move engine symbols across the abs.w/abs.l boundary and relocate the game bank. Budget them, and keep bank-keyed fixtures pins-derived per shape.** Same family as the paired-state-gate memo's "+0x78 bank growth colliding with pinned .emp placements". — RECORDED (class documented; all known fixtures hardened).

- [t24 pass-1 step 5, 2026-07-24] **sigil's AS front-end REJECTS bare conditional branches in `.asm` — the twin-explicit rule is mechanically enforced, not merely convention.** Writing `beq .done` in a twin assembles fine under asw (which auto-sizes to `.s`, byte-identically) but fails every sigil-side full-tree assembly with "branch needs an explicit size suffix (.s or .w) — Aeon pins branch width, no relaxation" (caught by `boot_port`'s twin-parity arms, which assemble the whole game through `sigil-frontend-as`). So the house rule "the .emp goes bare, the TWIN carries the explicit width" has a real enforcement mechanism on the twin side; a port that mechanically transliterates its bare `.emp` branches into the twin will build green under asw and red under sigil. — RECORDED (mechanism jot; explains why every twin in the corpus is explicitly sized).

- [t24 pass-2, 2026-07-24] **`let rN: Type` (body-position typed register) — DEMANDED-FEATURE CANDIDATE with its exact first call sites.** The loop's construct inventory lists `let rN: Type` under contracts, but the corpus has **ZERO instances** (`let` appears only for comptime values, vdp.emp:79-111), so t24 declined to make a byte-changing wave the construct's first use (overseer-accepted). The sites are named so the next tranche can take it deliberately: **`children.emp` `d2` in `CreateChild_Normal` / `_Complex` / `_FlipAware`** (each loads the child `code_addr` word from the ROM descriptor and stores it to `Sst.code_addr` — the exact value class already typed `ObjRoutine` at the two PARAM sites) and **`d4` in `CreateChild_Linked`** (the parked `code_addr` copy). All four are MOVED-and-STORED values, never arithmetic, so they type cheaply under the strict-degrade lattice; the win is that the file's one domain value would be typed at every surface instead of only where it crosses a proc boundary. Adoption is byte-neutral (types erase). Blocked on nothing but a deliberate first use. — RECORDED (candidate + sites).

- [t24 pass-2 D1, 2026-07-24] **Reader census for the deferred chain-head write (the gate-blind class, done on the record).** M5 hoists `move.w a2, sibling_ptr(a0)` out of three creator loops to one write per call, which is only safe if nothing can observe the parent's stale head mid-call. Census: the ONLY readers of a PARENT's chain head outside children are `sprites.emp:354` / `sprites.asm:321` (Render_Sprites' multi-sprite walk); the second read (`sprites.emp:404` / `sprites.asm:371`) walks a CHILD's next-sibling, not a head. `Render_Sprites` is called from the game state's main loop AFTER `RunObjects` returns (object_test_state.asm:124/162/262, ojz_scroll_test.asm:196, demo_state.asm:35), so a creator cannot be preempted by it; and NO interrupt path reads the field — `VInt_Level` only DMAs the sprite buffer that the previous `Render_Sprites` already built. Strictly, the deferral also NARROWS the theoretical window: the original wrote the head every iteration, so a hypothetical mid-call observer saw a partially linked chain, whereas the deferred write means the head only ever points at a completed run. Recorded because no byte gate can see any of this — both twins share whatever the answer is. — RECORDED (census; re-run it if a VBlank-side sprite build is ever introduced).

- [t24 pass-2, 2026-07-24] **The t24 ROM files shrink 90 bytes in BOTH shapes and NO byte table accounts for it — it is the convsym symbol-table appendix, not image shrink** (overseer catch; verified independently). Assembled `EndOfRom` is UNCHANGED in both shapes (plain `0x5DB60`, debug `0x5F65A` — `ASSEMBLED_LEN`/`DEBUG_ASSEMBLED_LEN` in pins.rs did not move), while file-length−image goes 37317→37227 plain and 38486→38396 debug: the whole −90 is the appendix, almost certainly M4's five deleted fail-skip label sets (fewer symbols to encode). Same class as t23's "+30 debug total = convsym re-encoding". **Say it explicitly in any packet and in the PROVENANCE re-baseline**, or the file sizes read as a 90-byte image shrink that the byte-delta table cannot explain (the engine block's own −0x6C plain / +0x46 debug is absorbed by the `org $10000` ObjCodeBase shield and never reaches the file length). — RECORDED (measurement note; the assembled lens is the one that means anything).

- [t24 pass-2 step 3(a), 2026-07-24] **ASK: derive the minimal save set from the callee's CONTRACT — the `save_across(call)` class.** The step-5 M1 cut (six alloc call sites) was pure contract arithmetic: `AllocDynamic` is `clobbers(d0) out(a1 if eq) preserves(a0)`, so everything else is contractually intact and the only register worth saving is the one the caller still needs that the callee writes. The LANGUAGE already holds every input to that computation (the declared contracts), yet the caller hand-computes the answer and then explains it in a four-line comment at every site — comment-as-compensation in its purest form (this file carries the note once and cross-references it five times). ASK: a construct that brackets a call and saves exactly the intersection of "live in the caller" and "written by the callee" — `save_across(AllocDynamic) { ... }`, or an attribute on the call that emits the movem pair. Wrong-input scan (macro-port rule): today a caller can oversave (silent waste — 44-116 cycles/child here, which is what the review caught), undersave (silent corruption, no diagnostic), or drift when the callee's contract changes (nothing re-checks the save set). All three become unrepresentable if the compiler derives it. Demand so far: 6 sites in children.emp + every hand-rolled save/restore pair in the corpus (`git grep 'movem.l.*-(sp)'` is the census). NOT built in-tranche: it needs liveness at the call site (the S2-D6 dataflow milestone), so it is step-4 verb (c) — a real design, not a stopgap. — RECORDED (ask, with the strongest demand data the class has).

- [t24 pass-2 step 5, 2026-07-24] **NOT TAKEN, with numbers: inlining `refresh_piece_count` at the six spawn sites.** The review's M2 observed that `PopulateSpawnedPieceCount`'s ~120-cycle call overhead (jbsr + movem pair + rts) wraps a ~40-cycle payload. Now that the body is a comptime template, inlining it at the six creators is a one-word change per site — and costs ~+24 bytes × 6 = ~+144 bytes of ROM to save ~120 cycles per child spawned. Spawn is an event path, not per-frame: at the object-test scenes' churn (a few spawns/frame) that is well under the LEAN threshold, while the ROM cost is permanent and the six copies would drift from the animator's single call. NOT TAKEN. Reopen if a spawn burst ever shows up in a profile (the emitter/churn regime is the place to look). — RECORDED (logged with numbers, per the LEAN bar).

- [t24 PANEL ROUND, 2026-07-24] **BUG (engine, out of t24 scope, surfaced to Volence): a parent's children are NEVER freed when the entity window despawns the parent — permanent dynamic-slot leak.** TWO VERIFIED FACTS: (1) `EntityWindow_DespawnObjects` calls `DeleteObject` on the parent only — `DeleteChildren` has exactly ONE caller in the whole tree (`test_parent.asm:165`); (2) the despawn walk tests `cmpi.b #SLOT_TAG_UNTAGGED, Sst.slot_tag(a0)` / `beq .next` and skips untagged slots, while `AllocDynamic` tags every popped slot `SLOT_TAG_UNTAGGED` — so every child is untagged BY CONSTRUCTION and can never despawn itself either. Consequence: an entity-window-spawned parent that spawns children and scrolls out leaks N dynamic slots for the level, and each orphan keeps dereferencing a `parent_ptr` into a freed or reallocated slot every frame. The existing orphan guard in `test_parent.asm:85-86` tests the POINTER, not the parent's liveness, so it never fires. THREE CANDIDATE FIXES: (a) despawn cascades — `EntityWindow_DespawnObjects` calls `DeleteChildren` before `DeleteObject` (cheapest, but adds a chain walk to the despawn path); (b) children inherit the parent's `slot_tag` so the window despawns them directly (changes what slot_tag means — it is the window's quadrant entry index, and two objects would share one); (c) `DeleteObject` clears the deleted object's children's `parent_ptr` / unlinks (needs the reverse mapping this SST layout does not have). This is a BUG row, not an optimization note. — **RULED (a) + FIXED + CLOSED 2026-08-02 (Volence, engine-debts opener):** `EntityWindow_DespawnObjects.despawn` now `jbsr DeleteChildren` before `DeleteObject` (aeon merge `8678ddb` / sigil merge `7e6ca108`, chain-23 `despawn-cascade`). Oracle A/B (overseer, foreground): pre-fix a window-despawned parent left its 3 children alive orbiting the zeroed corpse at origin FOREVER (end-state live=33, 3 slots never returned to the free stack); post-fix parent+children freed the SAME frame (free SP +8), reconcile 40→36, end-state 30-clean, free stack fully recovered, no walk-rail assert. Fixes (b)/(c) not taken per the ruling. Evidence: aeon `docs/superpowers/2026-08-02-engine-debts-opener-evidence.md`.

- [t24 PANEL ROUND, 2026-07-24] **Language ask: `preserves(rN)` should accept NEVER-WRITTEN as the strongest possible proof.** Lens C2 found a real verification blind spot — a proc's PARAM registers sit in the contract closure's `allowed` set (`lower/proc.rs`: "a write to a param register is part of the proc's own contract"), so if a callee ever started clobbering `a0`, none of children's six creators would fire a diagnostic even though every one of them reads `Sst.x_pos(a0)` after the call and the caller relies on `a0` afterwards. The natural close is for each creator to DECLARE `preserves(a0)` — but that is rejected: `[proc.preserves-unverifiable] … no save/restore round-trips its entry value on every return path`. The verifier demands a round-trip and does not accept "the body never writes it at all", which is a STRICTLY STRONGER proof than any save/restore pair. ASK: treat never-written as verified-preserved (the dataflow already computes the write set — this is a check that already knows the answer). — OPEN (verified by experiment; the fix as C2 proposed it does not compile today).

- [t24 PANEL ROUND, 2026-07-24] **Language ask: a conditional `out` on an otherwise-CLOBBERED register (the `AllocDynamic` a1 shape).** `AllocDynamic` declares `clobbers(d0) out(a1 if eq) preserves(a0)`, which under the exhaustive-license convention reads as "a1 survives unless eq". FALSE: the pop (`movea.w -(a1), a1`) happens before the full/latch tests, so both failure exits return with a1 destroyed. The honest form `clobbers(d0, a1) out(a1 if eq)` is REJECTED — `[proc.out-clobbers-overlap] … a register is either a returned result or destroyed scratch, not both`. But that is exactly the shape here: a1 is a RESULT on the eq edge and SCRATCH on every other. Overseer-ruled: do not fight the grammar mid-tranche — the truth ships as a site comment naming the failure edge (core.emp, this tranche) and the grammar gap is ledgered. ASK: let `out(rN if cc)` coexist with `clobbers(rN)`, meaning "result on the cc edge, destroyed otherwise". children.emp is the first caller to lean on this license as ARITHMETIC (its six-site save-set reduction) rather than prose, which is what made the falsehood matter. — **CLOSED 2026-08-04 (B'-0, contract delta spec §1).** A register carrying an `out(rN if cc)` guard is now subtracted from the `[proc.out-clobbers-overlap]` check (both CPU paths), because a result-on-one-edge / scratch-on-the-others register is not the result-or-scratch contradiction the diagnostic names. ROOT CAUSE (measured, not assumed — Fable's "likely closed by construction" hypothesis was WRONG and the pinning test caught it): `parser::out_list` lands an `rN if cc` register in BOTH `out_cond` AND the plain out reglist (out-verify needs it there), so the unconditional overlap loop saw it. `out ∩ preserves` still fires for a conditional result (written on ANY path contradicts untouched on ALL paths) — the wall is pinned by `uncond_out_still_may_not_overlap_clobbers`. `core.emp`'s `AllocDynamic` now declares the honest `clobbers(d0/a1) out(a1 if eq) preserves(a0)`; the ten-line CONTRACT CAVEAT that stood in for the grammar is replaced by a present-tense contract statement. Byte-identical ×6. — DONE.

- [t24 PANEL ROUND, 2026-07-24] **`save_across(call)` RE-SHAPED, not built (lens A1's adjudication accepted).** The pain is real — six sites hand-compute "callee license ∩ my live set" and explain it in prose — but the proposed construct is the wrong answer, for two reasons that survive scrutiny: (1) it cannot be derived from its stated input, because the register actually saved at all six sites is `a1`, which is NOT in `clobbers` at all (it is the conditional `out`, plus the undeclared failure-edge clobber above), so the derivation needs CALLER LIVENESS as well; (2) a derived save-set makes a REMOTE contract byte-affecting — editing `core.emp`'s declaration would silently change `children`'s region length and fire the five-site re-pin ripple from a file the editor never opened, which no existing template does. A1's third argument (that `find_dead_saves`/`destroys_value` already cover the class, observe-only) is PARTLY REFUTED by measurement: the dead-save worklist runs corpus-wide and reports **0 firings**, and it did not flag the dead `d0` save this panel found by hand, because it analyses saves against the proc's OWN body (d0 IS written inside), not caller-side liveness. So the diagnostic exists but does not cover the class. RE-SHAPED ASK: caller-side liveness in the contract layer — the check that would have found the `d0` oversave and would refuse an undersave — with the emitted save set left to the author. — OPEN (re-shaped).

- [t24 PANEL ROUND, 2026-07-24] **Sweep-enumeration accuracy: the step-6 list was wrong TWICE in one tranche, and a fresh lens caught it both times.** My enumeration named "camera.emp ×3" and the two `.asm` spellings; lens B2 found (a) two `.emp` sites in `load_object.emp:55-59` never named at all, and (b) that camera.emp has **five** promotion sites, not three — `:224-226` and `:319-321` spell the same pixels→16.16 promotion as `ext.l` + `lsl.l #8` ×2 (6 bytes instead of 4), which a `swap`-shaped grep cannot see. Plus `object_test_state.asm` ×4, `player_common.asm`'s `distToFix` MACRO (5 expansions — the corpus had independently named the same abstraction), and `test_player.asm:229-231`. **The lesson is the step-6 pattern-enumeration amendment's own failure mode:** grepping the shape you just built finds only the sites that already spell it your way, and "I updated the file at hand" masquerades as a sweep. A sweep must enumerate the SEMANTIC shape (here: any construction of a 16.16 value from integer pixels), not the syntactic one. — RECORDED (method note; the corrected enumeration is the step-6 input).

- [t24 FOCUSED RE-PANEL, 2026-07-24] **BUG CAUGHT IN OUR OWN WAVE: a priority BAND cannot be inherited with `or` — the band half of the render_flags inherit was shipped and reverted the same session.** The masked inherit (`or.b` of `RF_PRIORITY_MASK | 1<<RF_COORDMODE`) is correct for COORDMODE, which is a single flag bit and composes idempotently — but a BAND is a 3-bit VALUE in bits 5-7, and the engine-wide child-side idiom for setting one is `ori.b #N<<RF_PRIORITY_SHIFT`, which assumes a zeroed field. Or-ing an inherited band with the child's own therefore yields their bitwise UNION. VERIFIED against shipped test objects: `test_emitter.asm:21` sets band 5 (`$A0`) + COORDMODE (`$A8`); the inherit masks `$A8 & $E8 = $A8` into the effect; `test_particle.asm:20` then does `ori.b #6<<RF_PRIORITY_SHIFT` (`$C0`) → `$E8` → **band 7, not the intended 6**. `test_stress_emitter.asm:22` (band 7) puts its particles in band 7 as well, where `SPRITES_PER_BAND = 32` and the previously-single-object band starts exercising the overflow cascade. The band half was REVERTED (COORDMODE retained, mechanism and comment kept, the constraint stated at the const). **THREE FIX SHAPES for whoever rules on the band ruling: (a) change the child-side idiom to clear-then-set (`andi.b #~RF_PRIORITY_MASK` + `ori.b`) — a game-wide convention change; (b) inherit with `move.b` of the masked value AND accept that any child which sets its own band must clear first — same convention dependency, one instruction cheaper; (c) drop band inheritance and solve the real complaint (children default to band 0, the ONE band that cannot cascade — `sprites.emp:136-138` `subq.w #1,d0 / bmi .all_bands_full` — so a child-heavy scene saturates band 0 and drops sprites while bands 1-7 sit empty) by giving objects a spawn-time default band instead.** Note that for MULTISPRITE parents the band inherit was inert anyway: `Render_Sprites` emits chain-walked children under the parent's band entry and never re-bands them. — OPEN (band ruling needs re-deciding; COORDMODE half shipped and correct).

- [t24 FOCUSED RE-PANEL, 2026-07-24] **`children.asm`'s plain-shape `bsr.s PopulateSpawnedPieceCount` now sits at EXACTLY −128 — zero margin, and the failure mode is asymmetric.** The wave's 12-byte inherit block landed before that call in `CreateChild_Normal`; the displacement is now the last representable 8-bit branch. Any future edit adding ONE byte between the top of `PopulateSpawnedPieceCount` and that call breaks the plain build — and because the `.asm` widths are frozen literals while the `.emp` uses auto-reaching `jbsr`, the twins DIVERGE rather than both erroring, so only the byte gate catches it. Same file, second tripwire: `CreateChild_FlipAware`'s `bne.s .alloc_fail` has 3 bytes of slack. MITIGATION AVAILABLE AND UNTAKEN: `PopulateSpawnedPieceCount` still parks its one register with a `movem.l` pair; applying the same `move.l`/`movea.l` swap the wave applied to `DeleteChildren` shrinks it 38→34 bytes (−12 cycles per spawned child, on the hottest per-child path in the file) and restores 4 bytes of branch margin. — OPEN (both the margin note and the free −12 cycles).

- [t24 FOCUSED RE-PANEL, 2026-07-24] **Loop-invariant hoist available on the render_flags inherit — byte-neutral, turns the wave's biggest per-child cost into a saving.** The inherit reads and masks the PARENT's `render_flags` once per child, but nothing in any creator's loop writes it (`AllocDynamic` and `PopulateSpawnedPieceCount` both carry exhaustive `clobbers` licences that exclude a0's SST). Hoisting the read+mask to before the loop and leaving `move.b d1, Sst.render_flags(a2)` inside costs 8 bytes outside + 4 inside = the same 12 bytes, and takes the per-child cost from 36 cycles to 12. `d1` is free and already in the declared clobber set at five of the six creators (`CreateChild_Linked` has no free data register — it is `@scaffolding`, leave it). Related: `CreateChild_FlipAware` can fold its XFLIP set into the same hoisted value and delete its third `tst.w d4` (−14/−28 cycles per child, −10 bytes in the loop, which should also pull its two wave-promoted `.w` branches back to `.s`). — OPEN (post-band-ruling, since the mask's contents decide the shape).


- [t24 VOLENCE RULING, 2026-07-24] **POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT — priority-band inheritance for children (the FIRST member of this bucket; grep `POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT`).** Volence ruled the design goal ("children should have the parent's priority") STANDS, and ruled the WORK deferred to the to-do that runs **after the AS twins are gone** — his words: "jot it down in our to do after we get rid of twins". t24 therefore ships the COORDMODE half of the render_flags inherit and NOT the band half (reverted at the focused re-panel; live-verified at the merge gate — a freshly created child reads render_flags `$08`, i.e. coordinate-mode set with bits 5-7 still zero). Everything a later implementer needs, so nobody re-derives it: (1) **why `or` cannot do it** — a band is a 3-bit VALUE in bits 5-7, not a flag, so or-ing the parent's band with the child's own yields their UNION (measured: emitter band 5 + particle's own band 6 = band 7). (2) **The real blocker is a game-wide precondition, not a bug in the inherit** — TEN child-side sites spell `ori.b #N<<RF_PRIORITY_SHIFT` (test_particle, test_stress_emitter, test_churn, test_emitter, test_player, test_parent ×2, test_animated, player_common), each assuming a ZERO band field, and `engine/constants.asm:189` already documents the rule they lean on ("runtime priority changes must clear these bits first — `ori.b` alone accumulates stale bits", safe at spawn ONLY because the slot is zeroed). The inherit does not break a convention; it invalidates the precondition the documented convention depends on. (3) **The problem is worse than "children draw backmost"** — band 0 is the ONE band that cannot cascade (`sprites.emp` `.cascade` does `subq.w #1,d0` / `bmi .all_bands_full`), so a child-heavy scene DROPS sprites out of band 0 while bands 1-7 sit empty. (4) **Narrower payoff than it looks** — for RF_MULTISPRITE parents the inherit is inert: `Render_Sprites` emits chain-walked children under the PARENT's band entry and never re-bands them, so only independently-rendered children are affected. (5) **Three candidate shapes**, to be re-decided with the above in hand: (a) convert the ten child-side sites to clear-then-set (conformance to the documented rule) and then inherit with a masked `move.b`; (b) inherit but keep `or` and accept union semantics (rejected at t24 — it shipped a regression); (c) drop the inherit and solve the real complaint with a spawn-time DEFAULT band and/or a band-0 cascade fix in sprites.emp. **Why it waits for twin retirement:** (a) and (c) touch game-side objects and/or the PARKED sprites.emp, each edit currently pays the twin-lockstep + re-pin tax twice over, and the same work costs roughly half once the `.asm` twins are gone (one source file, no lockstep, no re-pin). Related in the same bucket: **children.emp's four `@scaffolding` zero-caller procs (CreateChild_Complex/_FlipAware/_Linked, CreateEffect_Simple) split to their own module at the same flip** — Volence's keep-and-mark ruling, with the split line recorded in the file; templating the 4-way creator clone (the `emit_piece_loop` skeleton-with-holes class) is natural to do in the same move. Also parked behind the band decision: the FlipAware XFLIP fold into the hoisted mask (row above). — OPEN.

- [t25 sound_debug lane, 2026-07-28] **`extern(...)` in a `lea`/instruction DISPLACEMENT expression does not parse — demand 1 (sound_debug mirror lane).** `lea (extern("SeqChannel_len") - SEQ_MIRROR_CHBYTES)(a0), a0` (a link-external base folded into a 16-bit displacement) errors "expected end of line" — the displacement-expression grammar rejects an `extern(...)` term. (A `moveq #extern("X")-1, d0` immediate DOES lower fine; the gap is the DISPLACEMENT form specifically.) sound_debug.asm's channel-skip `lea (SeqChannel_len-SEQ_MIRROR_CHBYTES)(a0), a0` needs it; blocks the sound_debug port. Workarounds if built later: a comptime `const` mirror of SeqChannel_len (with an `ensure` drift-guard against the extern truth, the TILE_SIZE precedent) sidesteps the extern-in-disp entirely. — RECORDED (demand 1; the sound_debug mini-tranche's first blocker).
- [t25 sound_debug lane, 2026-07-28] **PARSER ROBUSTNESS BUG: a parse error in operand/expression position inside an `asm` body can INFINITE-LOOP the parser (never returns) instead of erroring.** Triggered by the `extern(...)`-in-`lea`-displacement gap above, but only in ACCUMULATED context (a `{stop_z80()}` Code splice opening the body + a nested `.copy3c`/`.copy3cb` label loop + the module's `equ = extern(...)`/`ensure(...)` header) — minimization could not reduce it below ~26 lines (removing the splice, the nested labels, OR the header each makes the SAME trigger error cleanly), so the loop is context-sensitive and DEEP. Repro committed + `#[ignore]`d: `crates/sigil-frontend-emp/tests/parser_recovery_hang.rs` (`parse_str` never returns; run WITH A TIMEOUT). PARTIAL fix taken: `asm_body`'s statement loop got a zero-progress guard (force-advance + diagnose so THAT loop can never spin) — a real robustness improvement for the statement-loop class, but it does NOT fix this hang (the loop is DEEPER, in operand/expression parsing, before control returns to `asm_body`). A front-end must error loudly, never spin — so the deeper loop is a genuine bug to fix when the sound_debug mini-tranche returns. Kill: the repro's `parse_str` returns (a clean error) → un-ignore the test to assert it. — OPEN (demand 1; deep parser-recovery loop; repro + partial guard committed).

- [t25 error_handler pass-1 step-3(a)/4, 2026-07-28] **`exception_vectors` / labeled-raise TABLE construct — LOW priority, LOG not build.** The 12 exception-vector stubs are a structural clone (12 near-identical `pub proc X () { raise_exception "MSG"[, address_error] }`, ~36 lines differing only in the exported label + message + the address_error flag on 2). A table construct (`exception_vectors { BusError: "BUS ERROR", address_error; ... }` emitting N labeled procs each with a raise_exception) would collapse the ceremony — but it needs NEW GRAMMAR (a construct emitting distinct EXPORTED labels + bodies), i.e. verb (c) ask, not a small in-tranche build. Taste caveat: the explicit 12-stub form is arguably CLEARER (each CPU vector visibly named beside its message), and the lines-per-intent is modest (not the sfx_bank 197-for-9 class), so this earns no urgency. Build only if a second labeled-raise table appears (S3K/other games' vector tables are candidates) or the debug-runtime rewrite (POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT) wants it. — RECORDED (demand 1, low priority).

- [t25 error_handler panel A1, 2026-07-28] **`noreturn` / terminal-proc marker — language ask, demand 1.** The 12 exception-vector stubs (and the ErrorHandlerBlob) NEVER return — each ends in `jmp (pages).l` / is opaque data, no `rts`. The `pub proc X () clobbers()` signature says nothing about this; the design note calls the contract "nominal" and relies on the terminal `jmp` suppressing the fallthrough warning. A `noreturn` (or terminal) proc annotation would (a) document intent in the SIGNATURE not prose, (b) close the cold-reader gap A1 flagged (you can't tell a stub never returns from its header), and (c) let the contract lint treat clobbers as moot for a non-returning proc. Candidate consumers beyond error_handler: any exception/trap vector, `raise_error`-terminated procs, boot's entry. — RECORDED (demand 1, low-medium; grows if more vector/trap tables port). — **CLOSED 2026-08-05 by the noreturn-tail lane (spec `2026-08-05-noreturn-tail-design.md`).** `@noreturn` ships on the attrs channel (proc decls + `extern proc` sigs) as a CHECKED claim: `[noreturn.returns]` (error tier) refuses any `Return`/`FallOff` edge in the body — every path must leave by a transfer or a loop; the TRANSITIVE claim (the target never returns) is trusted like every declared contract. Adopted byte-neutral on the 12 exception-vector stubs + `ErrorHandlerBlob` (error_handler.emp), `GameLoop` (game_loop.emp), `EntryPoint` (boot.emp) — all 15 pass the check as-written. Consumers wired: cycle_budget (a `Defer` to a `@noreturn` target closes the path like a return); the CCR-bracket advisory (a User `jmp`/`jbra` to a `@noreturn` target is a divergent terminal, not a caller-visible CCR leave). Part (c) — clobbers moot for a noreturn proc — was NOT taken (spec §3: the "nominal" contracts stay untouched; the ledger-1068 widening convention is a separate question).
- [t25 error_handler panel C2, 2026-07-28] **`dc.l <label>` label_ctx moves a TYPO from an eval-time `unknown name` to a link-time `unresolved symbol`.** The new `lower_dc` label context (asm.rs) makes any unknown bareword a deferred link symbol (the `lower_ptr` precedent), so a misspelled const/label in a `dc` operand no longer errors at eval with "unknown name" — it fails LOUDLY at link as "unresolved symbol `<typo>`" instead. Still loud (not silently wrong), just later + a less precise message. Register-class words (d0-d7/a0-a7/sp/pc/sr/ccr) are carved out to keep the eval-time error. Same tradeoff `lower_ptr` already accepts corpus-wide. — RECORDED (accepted tradeoff; note only, no action).

- [t25 CLOSE — POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT, 2026-07-28] **debugger.asm RECLASSIFICATION home (grep `POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT`).** debugger.asm was NOT ported (it emits zero ROM bytes — DEBUGGER__* config equates + the macro tower `ifdebug/assert/RaiseError/Console/KDebug/__ErrorMessage/__FSTRING_*` + `padding off`/`supmode on`); its consumers are the `.asm` twins exclusively (the `.emp` side has its own diag construct). So the engine 68k conversion backlog drops it by RECLASSIFICATION, not port. But the macro tower + DEBUGGER__* config MUST stay AS-side while any `.asm` twin exists (the twins expand assert/RaiseError). At asl/twin retirement: (1) DEBUGGER__* config (SHOW_SR_USP, EXTENSIONS_ENABLE, the flag equates) needs an `.emp`-era home (a `const`/config module the diag construct reads — today they are hardcoded in diag.rs: `_eh_return`=$20, EXTENSIONS enabled-shape, address_error-only); (2) the macro tower dies with the twins; (3) **kill row 21's message-format/`b<cond>.w` pins are freed**, and (4) **kill row 53's raise_exception config-pin** (DEBUGGER__EXTENSIONS__ENABLE=1 / SHOW_SR_USP=0) is subsumed by the config module. — OPEN (POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT bucket member).
- [t25 CLOSE — POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT, Volence-ruled 2026-07-28] **Replace the vendored MD-Debugger blob with an OWN `.emp`-native diagnostics runtime (grep `POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT`).** Volence ruled 2026-07-28: the vendored handler (MD Debugger v2.6, (c) Vladikcomper — the 0xF56-byte opaque `dc.l` blob in error_handler.emp) is KEPT-FOR-NOW, replaced post-twin-retirement by an own runtime. Design seeds (from the t25 brief): (a) **Sigil emits the symbol table natively at link** → kills the convsym `deb2` appendix + its byte-gate allowlist ceremony (`assert_rom_matches_convsym`'s two-field derive) + the "region must be the final emission / no data after EndOfRom" constraint; (b) the diag construct **sheds the third-party FORMAT mirror** (the FSTRING encoder + the exit-flag parity rule in diag.rs, all pinned to debugger.asm's bytes) → **frees kill row 21's pins** and lets `raise_error`/`raise_exception` lower to a native format (kill row 53's config-pin dies with it); (c) the handler is **sized to the USED surface** — register dump + symbolized PC + backtrace; `Console.*`/`KDebug.*` have ZERO corpus sites (gap-ledger line ~995, demand 0) and Oracle MCP covers live debugging, so the on-screen handler's unique value is crashes on real hardware / foreign emulators; (d) the `dc.l <label>` capability (t25) + a native symbol-table emitter are the building blocks. Retires: the convsym appendix, kill rows 21/53, the vendored-binary-as-dc.l-wall ask, and the DEBUGGER__* config-home half of the row above. — OPEN (POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT bucket member; the largest single retirement dividend in the debug cluster).

- [codify-practiced-rules, Volence-ruled 2026-07-29 — POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT] **Full-corpus per-file retrospect once the twins retire (grep `POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT`).** After the `.asm` twins are gone (one source file, no lockstep, no re-pin — roughly half the ceremony), run a per-file retrospect over the ENTIRE `.emp` corpus against the BY-THEN-FINAL step-2 checklist + register-contract convention — every file gets: (1) convention compliance (the checklist as it finally stands, including every rule ratified AFTER a given file was ported — early files predate most of them); (2) the module-header-requirement audit (step-2 item 7 — role / gate+emission status / hardware-bank-timing contracts as C3's audit surface / hard-coded layout; a header making no checkable claim on a hardware-touching file is a miss); (3) contract re-derivation spot-checks (the closure gate over `clobbers`/`preserves`, honest-direction — step-2 item 9); (4) comment-hygiene (the exhibit-comment rule — present-tense contract fact, no change-history narration; the B6/B7 backlog folds in here, and the ~40-site codename backlog); (5) anything the campaign's LATER rules would have changed in early files (the drift the byte gate is structurally blind to — byte-identical spelling variants). ONE file at a time, byte-neutral expected (spelling/contract/comment only; a byte-changing find splits to its own gated parcel). Motivation: the 2026-07-29 convention-drift audit is the one-shot precedent — this is its standing successor, cheap once the twin tax is gone. — OPEN (POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT bucket member).

Do NOT fold them speculatively into an unrelated parcel. — OPEN (POST-TWIN-RETIREMENT companion; ties the audit backlog to the retrospect).

The configs differ (DEBUG+hotkeys · DEBUG+SOUND_DBG_MIRROR · no-sound), but the machinery (place `.emp` at a non-canonical config, byte-check vs the AS reference) is one and the same; one build serves all three. - [t26 DRY-PANEL A1+B1, 2026-07-29] **Typed/label DATA-TABLE construct — the tranche's highest-value ask (A1 A-1 + B1 finding-1 converged; the offset-table-roadmap #1 with two live instances).** vectors.emp wraps a pure 64-pointer CPU vector table in `pub proc Vectors () { dc.l <label> }` (a code construct emitting data, `clobbers()` meaningless) BECAUSE the corpus `data [*u8;N]` pointer arrays (mt_bank SongTable, tile_cache BlockStage_PtrTable) spell every cell as a quoted label-name string / `extern(...)` / `comptime for` — **none accept a BAREWORD link symbol**, and element 0 SYSTEM_STACK is an integer equ (heterogeneous with 63 label cells). Second instance: game_debug.emp's `Dbg_SfxIdTable: [u8; 8]` holds SFXID_* ids but stays raw `u8`, forcing an `as SfxId` RE-BLESS at the read site — a `[SfxId; 8]` typed array would carry the domain type table→slot. CONCRETE FRICTIONS probed this tranche: (a) `data [*u8;N]` cells reject barewords (corpus survey, B1); (b) a typed `data [SfxId; 8]` fails **"unknown type: SfxId"** in the isolated compile — the array-element newtype must be RESOLVABLE at the data-decl site, unlike the `as SfxId` bless which is opaque and lowers fine. The ask: a `data`-family construct whose cells take BAREWORD link symbols AND domain newtypes (vector/jump tables + typed id tables). Demand: 64 vector entries + 8 SFX ids + the MEMORY offset-table roadmap. VERDICT: vectors KEEPS proc-with-`dc.l` (t25-blessed, uniform int+label handling — category note added to the file); the construct is the LANGUAGE ask, not a t26 build. — RECORDED (ask, high demand; two live instances).
- [t26 DRY-PANEL A1, 2026-07-29] **Indexed/numbered data construct — vector-number is PROSE-ONLY (A1 A-2).** In vectors.emp each entry's identity is its position (vector N at offset N×4), but that identity lives ONLY in the trailing `// $NN:` comments — insert/drop one `dc.l` and every subsequent offset comment silently lies while the table still assembles (the region byte gate catches the size drift, but nothing IN-FILE ties an entry to its vector number). An indexed data construct (entry N asserted at offset N) would make the vector number structural. Same construct family as the typed/label-table ask above. — RECORDED (ask, folds into the data-table-construct family).
- [t26 DRY-PANEL A1+B1, 2026-07-29] **Hotkey-dispatch construct with comptime bit-disjointness — the input-handler shape (A1 B-2).** game_debug's Debug_MusicToggle re-proves the button-disjointness invariant in PROSE at 4 arms ("B ($10) does not collide with A($40)/START($80)", etc.) — a hand-maintained non-collision proof scattered across the file, exactly where the language fails to say in code what the comment asserts (nothing checks a future edit keeps the masks disjoint). A dispatch-on-distinct-mask-bits construct with a comptime bit-disjointness check would collapse all four prose proofs into one checked structure. B1: no shipped corpus construct fits (the 5× read/andi/beq chain is not-an-instance of anything today). Demand: 5 arms / 1 file, but the generic input-handler shape recurs in any future hotkey/menu handler. — RECORDED (ask; distinct from the mirror-const ceremony ask).

Cross-corpus: game_debug.emp, controllers.emp (raw `move.b Ctrl_1_Held`/`and.b`), engine.constants (BUTTON_* plain ints). A bigger design call than a single tranche (A4-i-adjacent, moved-and-compared bar); recorded as domain-scan demand data, NOT a t26 ask. — RECORDED (domain-type candidate, corpus-wide).
- [t26 DRY-PANEL C2, 2026-07-29] **C2 correctness lens: ALL CLEAN — no hazards.** Independently re-derived: the 64-long/256-byte vector table (entry-for-entry match, critical slots $00/$04/$70/$78 + all fills correct); the d0-d4/a0-a1 clobber closure is EXACT (Sound_PlayMusic's d0-d4/a0-a1 is the widest; no further a0 or other leak beyond the corrected d3/d4); button bits distinct + matching the twin; the SFX cycle wrap/RING-special-case/bounds/moveq-#0-clear all sound; no CC-clobber between test and Bcc; abs.w sign-extension of the $FFFF80xx RAM operands correct. The two neither-bucket catches (the extern-hidden transitive under-declaration; the demanded SongId/SfxId blesses) were the whole finding set. — RECORDED (panel correctness pass clean).

- [t26 STEP-6 CORPUS SWEEP, 2026-07-29] **Four new-class sweeps, per-site outcomes named:**

Verdict: its declared contract is the HONEST set, NOT a hand-comment trust — Sound_DebugMirror is a LEAF (no bsr/jsr, so no transitive closure to hide, unlike Debug_MusicToggle), and its body genuinely clobbers d0-d1/a0-a1 (d1 = `moveq #SEQ_MIRROR_CHANNELS-1, d1` channel-copy counter, d0 = inner byte counters, a0/a1 = copy pointers, none restored). The catch: the sound_debug.asm HAND COMMENT under-declared ("Clobbers: d0/a0/a1", missing d1) — the same comment-under-declaration shape game_debug surfaced, but here the extern got it right and the TWIN COMMENT was stale. NOT-AN-INSTANCE elsewhere; the noticing is a house-format proposal (step-2 item-2 exception-class candidate), not a retrofit.

## t27 (Z80 rung 1 satellites — phase 1 capabilities + lane A) — 2026-07-29

- **LEFTMOST local-label leaf in an immediate expression stays RAW (unmangled) — a real correctness bug.** A `.local` label as the FIRST operand token of an immediate expression (`ld de, .code_end + 1`, `move.l #.x+1`) is parsed as a raw `Sym(".code_end")` and never routed through `eval_local_label`'s owner-mangling, so the fixup targets a dangling `.code_end` the linker cannot resolve. A NON-leftmost occurrence (`ld bc, extern(...) - .code_end - 1`, `ld de, 1 + .code_end`) mangles correctly to `$m$Owner$code_end`. Proven by probe (`.x+1` → `Binary{Add, Sym(".x"), Int(1)}` vs `1+.x` → `Binary{Add, Int(1), Sym("$m$P$x")}`). Root cause is a PARSER asymmetry (a leading-`.` at operand start), so the fix is parser surgery — deferred (the LEAN balloons rule). WORKAROUND IN USE: z80_init.emp writes `ld de, 1 + .code_end` (byte-identical, `+` commutes), with a site comment. NEEDS: a parser fix so a leading-`.` local label at operand start is a proper local-label path; positive control = the byte-identical `.x+1`==`1+.x` pair, negative control = a dangling raw `.x`. Affects 68k too (not Z80-specific). — **CORPUS CENSUS (t27 step-6 sweep, 2026-07-29): ZERO live instances.** Swept every `.emp` for a leading-`.` local label at operand start followed by an arithmetic operator (the immediate-expression bug shape) — no hits. The one adjacent leading-`.`-at-operand-start site, `animate.emp:140 jmp .cc_table-4(pc,d0.w)`, is a PC-RELATIVE DISPLACEMENT (a different operand class), and it is BYTE-PROVEN correct by `animate_port`'s green gate — so the bug is specifically the IMMEDIATE / fixup-target path, NOT all operand-start local labels (pc-relative displacements with a leading-`.` local label resolve correctly). z80_init is the only file that would hit the immediate shape, and it uses the commuted `1 + .code_end` workaround. The bug stays latent (a hit would be a loud dangling-symbol link failure), scope now sharpened to immediate expressions.
- **Value16Le resident-cell census** (t27 step-6 sweep, 2026-07-29; ENUMERATE, do not port): the resident-Z80-address `dw <resident-label>` cell class (phase-1(c), landed for seq_opcode_tab) has exactly TWO more demand sites in the deferred Z80 corpus, BOTH in the GENERATED `engine/sound/sound_tables_z80.asm`: `PsgVolEnv_Ptrs` (`:76`, 11 cells) + `FmVolEnv_Ptrs` (`:106`, 3 cells) — `dw <resident-envelope-label>` pointer tables, the identical Value16Le class. The five resident CODE files (sequencer/sfx/fm/psg/driver) carry ZERO `dw <label>` data cells (SeqOpcodeTable was the only one, already extracted to seq_opcode_tab.asm — sound_sequencer.asm:1905 confirms the move). So the class's remaining demand lives entirely in the GENERATED file → owned by the generated-file/DSL row (the generator emits the `.emp`, not a direct port), CONVERGING a third time with that row. No rung 2-4 resident CODE file demands a new cell class for `dw` pointers.
- **No GLOBAL end-of-code label in a proc body** (feature-b C2): a bare `Global:` label inside a proc body is a parse error ("expected an expression, found Colon"). z80_init uses a trailing LOCAL `.code_end` (works, byte-identical, no external consumer). If a future consumer needs `Z80_IdleProgram_CodeEnd` EXPORTED, that is a global-label-in-body ask (parser surgery) — deferred; the trailing-local spelling is the standing form.
- **`sizeof()` on a data LABEL** yields "unknown type" (it expects a TYPE). The dac_sample_tab size guard uses a comptime `ensure(<declared length> == COUNT*stride, "…")` instead of an end-minus-start; noted so a future table-span guard uses the declared length / an end label, not `sizeof(label)`.
- **LANGUAGE ASK — a comptime SECTION-LENGTH / table-span primitive** (step-3(a), lanes B/C, 2026-07-29). BOTH ported tables carry an AS `if (End-Start) <> <expected> / error` span guard that measures the EMITTED byte span at assemble time: lane B `(SeqOpcodeTable_End - SeqOpcodeTable) <> 32*2`, lane C `(DacSampleTable_End - DacSampleTable) <> DAC_SAMPLE_COUNT*DacSample_len`. The `.emp` has no primitive that folds an emitted table's own byte span at comptime — no `sizeof(section)`, no comptime `end_label - start_label` (a trailing local label is a LINK-time value, cf. z80_init's `.code_end`, so it can't feed a comptime `ensure`). WORKAROUNDS IN USE: lane B drops the guard entirely (the AS twin's `if/error` stays canonical + the oracle length-check — its `32*2` has no doctorable extern anyway); lane C hardcodes the declared total `10*9` and drift-guards it against `extern("DAC_SAMPLE_COUNT")*extern("DacSample_len")` — which catches a COUNT/stride change but NOT the .emp emitting the wrong number of cells (a manual literal, unlike the AS `End-Start` which tracks emission automatically). THE ASK: a comptime span primitive (`section.len`, an emitted-byte counter, or a comptime-foldable in-section `here()`-delta) so a `.emp` data table can assert its own emitted size the way the AS `End-Start` guard does — the demand is 2 files / 2 span guards so far, a data-table-DSL-adjacent feature. DEFERRED (LEAN; own design + build).
- **Generated-file ownership class** (carried, brief §1): `engine/sound/sound_tables_z80.asm` is `tools/gen_sound_tables.py` output, included both resident and banked — porting a generator's output is the wrong seam; the real conversion is the generator emitting `.emp` (or the data-table DSL single-sourcing it). Do NOT port the .asm; owned as a toolchain decision. **CROSS-REF (2026-07-29):** this is the SHARED REAL FIX for blocker 1 of the whole-ROM banked-head seam row below — the lane C SND_* comptime-source circularity dissolves once a generator/data-table-DSL single-sources the SND_* build-time constants into `.emp` (rather than the .emp folding AS-side `=` addresses). The two rows converge on one toolchain step.
- **Whole-ROM CANONICAL banked-head placement seam (lanes B/C scale 2) — STOPped, DEFERRED sub-tranche** (2026-07-29). Lanes B/C are canonical bytes in `main.asm:201-217`'s `phase 08000h` head (the last two `soundBankHead` includes; `sound_bank.inc:36-37`); a real port must gate the includes, place the `.emp` at the phased head LMA (`MovingTrucks_Bank_Start + head_offset`, `vma:$8000+off`), advance the AS phase cursor past the span (t23/kill-row-46 class), region-pin (P4). Scale (1) windowed oracles landed (proving P1/P2/P5 on the real files); scale (2) STOPped per the STOP-if-no-clean-seam rule. FOUR blockers (§10 of `2026-07-29-t27-step0-design.md`): (1) **lane C SND_* comptime-source** — the decisive gap: 30 address-derived build-time constants with no clean comptime source in a whole-ROM build (`-D` is circular, a mirrored twin is 30 values + 30 drift guards); **CONVERGES with the generated-file ownership row above (the SHARED REAL FIX): a generator/data-table-DSL single-sourcing the SND_* into `.emp` dissolves this blocker — solve that toolchain step first and this seam's lead blocker is gone.** (2) lane B's 26 `Seq_Op_*` resident symbols need cross-frontend export from `sound_sequencer.asm` (a forbidden-to-touch resident file — read only). (3) an UNPROBED placement mode: a `vma:$8000` phased head table LMA-pinned inside the `$58000` bank (neither `z80_init.emp`'s `vma:$0` nor `dac_samples.emp`'s `bank:$8000` matches). (4) the phased org-advance resume is a window address depending on the preceding includes' sizes. Distinct from the DEMAND-3 off-canonical no-sound family (this is the CANONICAL head, not the no-sound build). Owned as its own future step-0 seam design + dual-build byte check.
- **STEP-4 CONSTRUCT LOG (LOG-ONLY per the brief LEAN bar, lanes B/C, 2026-07-29)** — neither table adopts a construct this tranche; both logged with reasons: (a) **lane B `seq_opcode_tab`** — 32 `dc.w <resident-label>` is a DENSE ABSOLUTE-pointer array. `offsets` does NOT fit (it is `dc.w Target-Base` SELF-RELATIVE, dense-ordinal — these are absolute `Value16Le` cells); `table` (D2.36) does NOT fit (counted/sentinel/SPARSE keyed — this is dense, unkeyed). A raw `dc.w` list IS the right spelling for a dense absolute Z80 jump table; nothing to build. (b) **lane C `dac_sample_tab`** — 10 uniform 9-byte descriptor records is a DENSE UNIFORM RECORD LIST. **The closest-fitting corpus idiom is the `act_descriptor.emp` STRUCT-ARRAY form (panel B1 finding, 2026-07-29):** a `DacSample` struct twin (in `engine/structs.emp`, joining Act/Sec/DMAEntry — the ROM-descriptor-struct-twin class, drift-guarded vs `sound_constants.asm`'s `DacSample_*`) + `pub data DacSampleTable: [DacSample; 10] = [ dac(BANK,PTR,LEN), … ]` + a `comptime fn dac(...) -> DacSample` filling the fixed `ds_rate=0`/`ds_codec=0`/`ds_loop_ofs=0` fields. WINS: deletes the 30 repeated `dc.b 0`/`dc.w 0` boilerplate rows (each row names only its 3 varying fields); makes the span guard STRUCTURAL via `[DacSample;10]` + `sizeof(DacSample)` (a type-level count + stride, closing the hand-literal `10*9` gap noted above); adds a per-field drift wall vs `sound_constants.asm` the byte-count `ensure` lacks. **DEFERRED THIS TRANCHE — code-tier, NOT trivially-takeable (overseer step-4 ruling):** it is FIRST-OF-KIND for a struct-typed data literal in a `(cpu: z80)` section — no corpus/test exercises struct-data under Z80 (every struct-data precedent, incl. act_descriptor, is `cpu: m68000`; these two files are the corpus's first `cpu: z80` DATA sections at all). Its parts are proven separately (`z80_section_serializes_little_endian` proves `u16` LE in a Z80 section; act_descriptor proves `[Struct;N]` on 68k) but the COMPOSITION is unproven → a demanded-feature capability step (TDD the Z80 struct-data path + build the struct twin + the `dac()` fn + re-derive the oracle), which the LEAN LOG-ONLY bar defers. RE-OPENS row 991's "dac_samples NOT a candidate (it was .asm-side)" now that the table IS .emp-side. Its natural home is the same seam as the whole-ROM scale-2 port (the struct twin wants structs.emp; the SND_* still need a comptime source). (c) **TYPE LAYER (LOG-ONLY per brief §5):** the cells carry Z80-side domain values — `Seq_Op_*` handler ADDRESSES (a resident-code-pointer type), `SND_*_BANK` (bank id), `SND_*_PTR` (a `$8000`-window pointer), `SND_*_LEN` (byte count); a `DacSample` struct would type the whole descriptor. Z80-side newtypes are the recon's demand class, deferred to rungs 2+; logged as candidates, not built.
- **CLOSED by phase 1**: the Z80 expression-imm16 defer (was "T1 §1.1/§4 over-claim") — LANDED (sigil `1c7336a`). The resident-Z80-address `Value16Le` value cell (was the `lower/data.rs:184` "T6 Abs16Le" deferral, ledgered ~825-831 "unprobed on Z80") — LANDED (sigil `64c2e12`), closing the "Z80 LE data unprobed" row for the width-2 local-ref case.
- **Z80 width-1 `db` cannot carry a LINK symbol; comptime constants need a comptime source** (lane C, 2026-07-29). Two composed gaps force the DAC descriptor table's SND_* cells to be COMPTIME constants (`-D` defines), NOT equ carriers as the brief §1 lane C assumed (TREE WINS, deviation flagged): (a) a bareword/`dc.b <link-symbol>` in a Z80 section routes through `fixup_kind(Z80, 1, false)` → `[cross-cpu.unwindowed-pointer]` (a 1-byte Z80 pointer is unrepresentable — correct: a Z80 address is 16-bit); (b) `extern("X")` returns a `LinkExpr` residual, which `dc` rejects as `[dc.comptime-only]` (dc takes comptime ints / strings / bareword link labels, not a `LinkExpr`). So an AS-side `db SND_BLIP_BANK` (a comptime `=` constant the twin folds) ports as a bareword comptime cell — resolved from a `-D` define (the sound-migration T2 mechanism) or a mirrored local `const` (the sound_api row-10 drift-guard class), NOT `extern()`. The `dc.w` cells (ptr/length, width 2) COULD route `Value16Le` as link symbols, but they are constants too, so all cells stay comptime for uniformity + correctness. SCALE-2 IMPLICATION: a whole-ROM lane C port needs the SND_* as comptime values at the .emp — either `-D` defines computed by the build (circular: the values derive from sample addresses only known after assembling dac_samples) or a mirrored-const twin (30 consts + 30 drift guards). This is a genuine scale-2 seam complication, distinct from placement.
- **CLOSED by lane C scale (1)** (2026-07-29): P1 (Z80 little-endian data) proven for COMPTIME-CONSTANT cells on the REAL `dac_sample_tab.asm` — the 10-descriptor / 90-byte table lowers byte-identical to the AS twin (`dac_sample_tab_port`), with the 30 SND_* as `-D` defines. P5 (the `if/fatal` size guard has a comptime `.emp` equivalent that FIRES on a doctored size) proven: `ensure(10*9 == extern("DAC_SAMPLE_COUNT") * extern("DacSample_len"), "…")` defers to a `LinkAssert`; a doctored `DAC_SAMPLE_COUNT` equ carrier (11) makes the guard fire at link (`doctored_dac_sample_count_fires_size_guard`), the undoctored count passes (positive control's partner). The comptime int in a Z80 `dc.w` emits little-endian (`encode_scalar` forces LE when `cpu==Z80`, `le:false` notwithstanding).
- **CLOSED by lane B scale (1)** (2026-07-29): P1 (Z80 little-endian data) + P2 (a banked `(cpu:z80, vma:$8000)` section whose cells are resident phase-0 addresses) proven on the REAL `seq_opcode_tab.asm` — the 32-entry `dc.w <resident-label>` table lowers byte-identical to the AS twin via the windowed oracle (`seq_opcode_tab_port`), with the ~26 `Seq_Op_*`/`Seq_BadOpcode` handler addresses as equ carriers. t24 positive control (one doctored handler address diverges) + both-sides-equal control. This fully closes the ~825-831 "Z80 LE data unprobed on Z80" row at FILE scale for the symbolic-cell case. NOTE: `seq_opcode_tab`'s span guard (`if (End-Start) <> 32*2 / error`) has NO doctorable extern (pure `32*2` literals), so it has no meaningful `.emp` comptime equivalent — the entry-count invariant stays enforced by the canonical AS twin's `if/error` + the oracle length check (the `sizeof`-on-label / table-span-guard gap above applies; the count is not a doctored-extern P5 case).

## t28 (parser debt + DEMAND-3 machinery + sound_debug) — 2026-07-29

- **CLOSED — P1 (deep parser-recovery loop, row ~1583).** Root cause was NOT "deeper in operand/expr parse" (the t25 note's guess); it was the TOP-LEVEL `recover_to_next_decl`: `extern` sits in the declaration OPENERS list but is a CONTEXTUAL opener (only `extern proc` is an item), so recovery STOPPED on a bare `extern` (the `equ X = extern(...)` header lines), `item()` re-failed, recovery re-stopped — infinite loop. Fixed by extending the existing contextual-opener guard (ensure/ensure_fatal/align) to skip a non-`proc` `extern` (sigil `a375526`). The t25 `asm_body` zero-progress guard is a separate defense, kept. Repro un-ignored (`recovery_terminates_on_extern_disp_in_context`) + minimal direct regression added.
- **CLOSED — P2 (extern-in-lea-displacement, row 1582).** The gap was the WRAPPED-PAREN form `(disp)(An)` for ANY displacement expr (the extern was incidental — the unwrapped `disp(An)` already parsed). The `Tok::LParen` operand arm only checked for a trailing `+` (post-inc), never a trailing `(`. Fixed: a single-part size-free paren group followed by `(` becomes a displacement over the following indirect (sigil `feabcfb`). NOTE: the sound_debug demand site ALSO needed the displacement base to be a COMPTIME const (the const-mirror, below) — P2 enables the parse, the const enables the fold; both are needed. The `.field`-in-arithmetic disp remains a separate LOWERING gap (unchanged).
- **CLOSED — P3 (leftmost-local-label immediate, row 1610).** `eval_path`'s label fallback returned a RAW `Value::Label(".x")` for a leading-`.` single-segment path, while the interior `Expr::LocalLabel` mangled via `eval_local_label`. Fixed in shared `eval_path` (a dotted name short-circuits to `eval_local_label`, CPU-agnostic; sigil `6c2c19b`). Disp/pc-relative/branch paths use `scope.resolve_ref` (pre-eval), untouched → byte-neutral (census: 0 live instances). Positive control `.x+1`==`1+.x` (Z80 imm16); negative control (undefined leftmost is loud). The t27 commuted `<const> + .local` spelling is now OPTIONAL (step-2 item 5 amended).
- **DEMAND-3 whole-ROM off-canonical machinery — LANDED (t28 lane M).** The harness capability to build the WHOLE ROM mixed (asl + sigil-gated `.emp`) at an OFF-CANONICAL config and byte-compare against a pure-`assemble_root` build at the SAME config. Home: `crates/sigil-harness/tests/mixed_offcanonical_rom.rs`. Reference = pure `.asm` through sigil's AS front-end (both sides PRE-convsym → `assert_rom_matches` with EMPTY allowlist, NOT `_convsym`; overseer ruling 1, empty allowlist is a HARD BAR). Three consumers / two configs, all green by name: `mixed_game_debug_config_a` + `mixed_combined_config_a` (game_debug + sound_debug, Config A = __DEBUG__+SOUND_DRIVER_ENABLED+SOUND_DEBUG_HOTKEYS+SOUND_DBG_MIRROR), `mixed_z80_init_config_b` (Config B = no-sound). Window oracles KEPT (ruling 2, additive not replacement). Closes the t26 paired-machinery row (~1512(c)) + the game_debug no-gate-arm deviation. Gate arms added: SIGIL_EMP_GAME_DEBUG (main.asm), SIGIL_EMP_SOUND_DEBUG (engine.inc); SIGIL_EMP_Z80_INIT else-arm gained `org $3FE` + numeric `Z80_IDLE_SIZE`.
- **Config B debug+no-sound DELIBERATELY UNEXERCISED (ruling 3, LEAN).** Only the plain no-sound shape is gated; the boot_data.asm z80_init else-arm is shape-shared. If a future debug-only divergence appears in that arm, this row is the tripwire — add a debug Config-B gate then.
- **sound_debug.emp const mirrors SEQ_CHANNEL_LEN(=60)/SND_SEQ_TRACE_LEN(=32) — the TILE_SIZE class (kill row 44).** SeqChannel_len/SND_SEQ_TRACE_LEN are COMPTIME constants the AS twin folds (a struct length + a =32 equate); a link-external `extern()` cannot size a d16 displacement / moveq imm, so the `.emp` mirrors them with `ensure(extern == const)` drift guards (exactly as ledger row 1582 predicted). 2nd `.emp` consumer of either name → hoist to a shared twin.
- **HOIST-SOMEDAY (B1 panel note, NOT a STOP):** `mixed_offcanonical_rom.rs`'s file-local `aeon_dir`/`strict_gate`/`placed_emp`/`flatten_with_asserts` (and the same boilerplate in mixed_dac_rom.rs / game_debug_port.rs / z80_init_port.rs) could join sigil-harness lib. The new file's parameterized `placed_emp` is already a cleaner form than mixed_dac_rom's per-tranche copies. Consolidation deferred (entrenched per-file convention; not this tranche).

## t28 merge-gate step-6 sweeps (overseer-ordered) — 2026-07-29

- **HARNESS: two separately-lowered `.emp` modules cannot be combined in one link (the flip-harness `sec0` collision).** Attempting the fully-`.emp`-to-`.emp` kill-row-42 flip (compile `vblank.emp` + `sound_debug.emp` together so `jbsr Sound_DebugMirror` resolves module-to-module) failed: each module lowered separately produces its own default `sec0` section at LMA 0x0, and extending one section list with the other's collides ("sections `sec0` [0x0,0x2) and `sec0` [0x0,0x2) overlap — colliding pins"). REPRO: `lower_module` each of two `.emp` files, concat the section lists, `resolve_layout` → overlap error. WORKAROUND: the combined WHOLE-ROM off-canonical gate (`mixed_offcanonical_rom::mixed_combined_config_a`) proves the byte-exact cross-seam call (AS-vblank → placed emp-sound_debug) — a STRONGER artifact than the isolated flip. Only bites tests that combine two independently-lowered `.emp` modules at a synthetic base (the window-oracle flip pattern); the whole-ROM path (one AS module + placed emp sections) is unaffected. KILL: a harness helper that renames/relocates each module's default section (or the frontend gives an unnamed section a module-scoped name), OR Spec-5 (no synthetic-base flip tests once the reference is sigil's own link). — RECORDED (harness limitation; overseer-ordered row).
- **DIAGNOSTICS: a body-position `{fn()}` Code-splice gives the cryptic `expected an instruction, found LBrace`.** A `{stop_z80()}`-style splice is valid ONLY inside a splices-allowed context (a `comptime fn` template body or an `asm {}`/`sr_masked(asm{})` block); at a bare PROC-body statement position the correct form is `fn()` (no braces). The current error ("expected an instruction, found LBrace") does not steer the author to either fix, and CONTRIBUTED to the t25 sound_debug draft sitting broken for days (the splice error was masked by the parser hang, then read as a mystery). THE ASK: when `{` opens a statement where splices are not allowed, diagnose "`{expr}` Code-splices are only valid inside a comptime-fn / `asm {}` template; at proc-body statement position use `fn()` (no braces)". Corpus census (sweep 2): every live `{fn()}` site (sound_api.emp ×4, inside `sr_masked(asm{})`) is a VALID splice context; sound_debug.emp uses the bare `stop_z80()` form; ZERO body-position misuses remain. — RECORDED (diagnostics polish; demand 1).

### t29 panel (A1) — two corpus-wide type-layer candidates (LEDGER, not t29 changes)

- **`objroutine(label) -> ObjRoutine` typed constructor (item-13 candidate).** Panel
  A1 noticed the asymmetry: test_animated types its VRAM values through constructors
  (vram_art→VramArtTile, the new vram_bytes→VramAddr) but stores the dispatch word as
  raw arithmetic `#TestAnimated_Main - ObjCodeBase` into the `ObjRoutine`-typed
  code_addr field — defeating the "never arithmetic" newtype at the one store that
  matters. This is CORPUS-WIDE, not t29-local: test_solid.emp:22 / test_particle.emp:35
  (the shipped siblings) inline the identical difference, and the inline was the
  deliberate C1 ruling (bare label-difference over a helper). An `objroutine(l) ->
  ObjRoutine` comptime fn (parallel to vram_art) would type the construction, but it
  is a corpus-wide sweep (every object module) that REVERSES the C1 inline decision —
  so it is a step-6-class corpus retrofit + a design call, not a t29 file change.
  LEDGERED as the item-13 ObjRoutine-construction candidate; reconciles the A1
  asymmetry finding. **DEMAND += 6 (t30 G2): 3 → 9.** The three effect/child objects
  add 4 code_addr-STORE sites (`#X - ObjCodeBase` into code_addr: test_emitter,
  test_stress_emitter, test_churn ×2 — the replacement-self store on a1) PLUS 2
  DATA-position sites (the EffectSpawn1 descriptor `code: extern("TestParticle") -
  extern("ObjCodeBase")`, which a `dc.w objroutine(...)`-in-data would also serve —
  see the `dc` link-expr ledger row). The data-position sites RAISE the constructor's
  value: an `objroutine(l) -> ObjRoutine` usable in BOTH a store immediate and a `dc`
  cell would close the `dc`-link-expr gap too (one construct, two demands).
- **AnimId / MappingFrame `$FF` spawn sentinel as a named const (item-13, low pri).**
  test_animated stores `#$FF` into prev_anim (AnimId) / prev_frame (MappingFrame) as
  the "no previous / force refresh" sentinel (documented in sst.emp). Same in every
  shipped object (test_particle.emp). A named `AnimId`/`MappingFrame` "none" const
  would name the sentinel, but A1 itself flags it as near-ceremony under the LEAN bar,
  and it is corpus-wide (not t29-local). LEDGERED, low priority; not built.

- [tranche 30 G2 step-1, 2026-07-29] **imm16 link-deferral SHIPPED (demanded feature)** — the AS-side game-object SPAWN idiom `move.w #objroutine(EffectObj), SST_code_addr(a1)` (object_test_state.asm:54/228, spawning the now-`.emp`-owned TestStressEmitter/TestChurnObj) needs the AS front-end to defer an UNRESOLVED 16-bit immediate to link, exactly as R3's `try_defer_long_imm` did for imm32. t29's outbound consumer was a `dc.w` (data, already deferred by `directive_dw`); t30 is the FIRST tranche to gate out an object referenced by a `move.w #` IMMEDIATE, surfacing the gap. `try_defer_long_imm` (eval.rs) generalized to size W → `Value16Be` fixup; scoped to MEMORY destinations (the `(An)`/disp-0-`(An)` spawn shape) — register-dest word immediates stay LONG-only (R3's boundary; `move.w #Sym,d0` still errors). The disp-0 `SST_code_addr(a1)` dest MUST fold to `(An)` (mode 2) in the deferral, since the eager path's zeroOffsetOptimization fold runs AFTER `lower_inst` (which the deferred frag skips) — else the imm hole ships an extra `$0000` disp word (+2 B, ROM drift). Tests: `imm32_defer::move_w_unresolved_symbol_{to_ind_defers_as_value16be, to_disp0_folds_and_defers}` (defer + fold + link byte-match) + the whole-ROM `mixed_tranche30` arms exercise it end-to-end. Full strict suite green except the churn/a0 blocker below (zero regression from this change). — SHIPPED.
- [tranche 30 G2 step-1, 2026-07-29] **`dc` cannot hold a link-EXPRESSION (`extern-extern`) — worked around via a typed record** — the effect emitters' `.particle_desc` descriptor spells its objroutine word as `dc.w TestParticle - ObjCodeBase` (a link-time difference, cross-module). sigil's `dc` accepts a comptime int, a string, or a bare LINK SYMBOL — but NOT a link-EXPRESSION (`[dc.comptime-only]`). The corpus has NO `dc.w Label-Label` precedent (every data-position code_addr goes through a typed struct field, e.g. objdef's `ObjDef.code_addr: ObjRoutine`). WORKAROUND (shipped, sigil-native, arguably better): a file-local `struct EffectSpawn1 (size:6) { code: ObjRoutine @0, x_off: i8 @2, y_off: i8 @3, term: u16 @4 }` + `data <name>: EffectSpawn1 = EffectSpawn1 { code: extern("TestParticle") - extern("ObjCodeBase"), ... }` — the ObjRoutine field carries the link-expr (struct fields DO, unlike raw `dc`). Byte-identical to the AS blob. THE ASK (demanded-feature candidate): let `dc` hold a bounded link-expression (`sym - sym`/`sym ± const`) so the raw descriptor form lowers, OR an `objroutine(label) -> ObjRoutine` comptime constructor usable in `dc` position (the item-13 candidate). Every future data-driven spawn descriptor (test_parent's G3 14-byte format, all children-descriptor tables) hits this. — RECORDED (workaround shipped; the typed-record form is the standing spelling until a `dc` link-expr or objroutine-in-data lands).
- [tranche 30 G2, 2026-07-29] **BLOCKER (STOP-finding): `verify_preserved` cannot prove TRANSITIVE preservation through a preserving call before rts** — TestChurnObj_Main is dispatched as `ObjRoutine = proc (a0: *Sst) preserves(a0, d7)` (core.emp:21), so it MUST preserve a0; it DOES (net) — the expiry path stack-saves self, restores it (`movea.l (sp)+, a0`), then self-deletes via `jsr DeleteObject` (which `preserves(a0)`, core.emp:205), then `rts`. `verify_preserved` (preserves.rs) is LOCAL: on ANY call it conservatively clobbers all registers (line 349-351), so the trailing `jsr DeleteObject` — AFTER the restore, BEFORE rts — makes a0 unprovable (it cannot credit DeleteObject's `preserves(a0)`). So: declaring `preserves(a0)` → `[proc.preserves-unverifiable]` ERROR (breaks the byte-gate lowering, where DeleteObject is a synthetic contract-less callee); NOT declaring it → `contract_closure_corpus::corpus_closure_residue_is_empty` fires (`[("TestChurnObj_Main","a0")]`, a0 written+undeclared) AND the ObjRoutine `preserves(a0)` bound is unmet. `clobbers(a0)` is forbidden (violates the ObjRoutine bound). The churn is the FIRST ported object Main that WRITES a0 (save/restore self-management) — every prior object leaves a0 read-only (trivially preserved). THE FIX (Fable/contract-system design): thread the corpus's callee-DECLARED-preserves into `verify_preserved` so a call to a proc declaring `preserves(rN)` does not clobber rN — spanning the per-file lowering check (where the callee's contract may be an unresolved synthetic → defer, not error) and the corpus closure (where it's known). Cross-phase; touches the contract system's core. Port is otherwise byte-complete (test_churn byte-identical both shapes, windowed + whole-ROM). Overseer ruling needed before the churn's contract can close. — OPEN (checkpoint-(a) STOP).

### t31 G3 (test_parent + the SpawnDesc hoist), 2026-07-29

- [tranche 31 G3 step-1, 2026-07-29] **GAP: a bare LOCAL label in a `data`/struct-field position cannot combine with a link-time `extern` via `-`** — test_parent's child descriptor needs each entry's `code` word = `TestChildPart - ObjCodeBase` (a same-module `pub` label minus the cross-seam extern base). Spelled with the bare local label (`code: TestChildPart - extern("ObjCodeBase")`) it errors `[here.provisional] a label value cannot combine with a link-time here() value` — a local label in a data literal is a `here()`-provisional (section-relative, address not yet fixed), and `-` is undefined between a provisional-here value and a link-time extern. WORKAROUND (shipped): reference the local label THROUGH `extern()` too — `code: extern("TestChildPart") - extern("ObjCodeBase")` — forcing link-time resolution of a label that is in fact local-and-`pub` (the linker resolves it against all sections, same-region included). Byte-identical. This generalizes the emitters' cross-region form (`extern("TestParticle") - extern("ObjCodeBase")`) to a same-module label. THE ASK: let a bare local `pub` label combine with an extern in data position (resolve the provisional at link like the instruction-immediate path already does — `#TestChildPart_Main - ObjCodeBase` works fine in a `move.w #` immediate), OR the `objroutine(label) -> ObjRoutine` constructor (item-13) usable in `dc`/struct-field position closes this alongside the t30 `dc`-link-expr gap. — RECORDED (workaround shipped; the `extern()`-on-a-local spelling is the standing form until the constructor or the data-position provisional-combine lands).

### t31 G3 dry-panel (A1 + B1 + C2), 2026-07-29

- [t31 panel A1, 2026-07-29] **CONFIRMED latent defect → NAMED VOLENCE OVERRIDE FLAG: test_parent's self-destruct + DeleteChildren cascade is UNREACHABLE — the object cannot demo the behavior it is named for.** TestParent_Main uses `TParentV.life_timer` for BOTH the self-destruct countdown (`subq.w #1` / `bne`, then `jbsr DeleteChildren` / `jbra DeleteObject`) AND the swing-phase counter — and the left-swing branch RESETS it to PARENT_LIFETIME (test_parent.emp:200) every cycle. Traced: life_timer runs 180→120 (swing right) → sets x_dir=1 at 119 → 118→60 (swing left) → at post-subq 59 resets to 180. It NEVER reaches 0, so the `subq→0→bne`-guarded self-destruct path (:177-179) is dead code; the parent oscillates forever and the DeleteChildren cascade (the object's stated purpose, header :1-2) never fires. FALSE comment claims at :1 ("then self-destructs"), :167-168 ("eventually self-destruct"), :177 ("Timer expired"). FAITHFUL to the AS twin (test_parent.asm:189/200 identical reset + same claims) — a PRE-EXISTING latent defect the cold read surfaced, NOT a t31 port regression; the byte gate cannot see it (both twins agree). RESOLUTION IS AN OVERSEER/VOLENCE RULING (comment-vs-code authority): (a) comment authoritative → oscillates-forever by design, correct the 3 comments byte-neutrally; (b) code authoritative → the object is NAMED to demo the cascade, so separate the phase counter from the life countdown (byte-CHANGING behavior fix, lockstep twin + oracle-verify). Not silently resolved — flagged at checkpoint (b). — RULED 2026-07-29 (overseer): (a) COMMENTS CORRECTED byte-neutrally in both twins (present-tense, mechanism stated — aeon `4cc61cf`), CODE AUTHORITATIVE FOR BYTES; the behavior fix (separating the phase counter from the life countdown) is DELIBERATELY NOT TAKEN — it joins the VOLENCE OVERRIDES list as a NAMED FLAG (his test scene; the ObjectTest soak population dynamics may anchor determinism baselines, so a lifecycle change could silently invalidate them — the t24 never-freed-children pattern: confirmed defect, ledgered with the full trace above, HIS CALL). — FLAGGED (Volence override). — RESOLVED 2026-07-29: Volence ruled FIX; landed as fix-test-parent-lifecycle (aeon merge 3d0337e): TParentV gains swing_phase u16 @2 (exact swing math preserved frames 1-179), life_timer = pure countdown, the cascade FIRES (oracle A/B: 3 parents + children expire ~3s, slots reclaimed, scene healthy; soak note: the parent/child lifecycle is now a one-shot demo — a future soak wanting persistent parent pressure re-spawns or extends PARENT_LIFETIME).
