### t32 psg port (checkpoint a), 2026-07-29
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **Z80 bare-comptime-symbol in an 8-bit immediate / ALU / bit-number operand SHIPPED (demanded feature)** — the psg corpus is the FIRST Z80 CODE file with SYMBOLIC 8-bit immediates (`sub CHROUTE_PSG1`, `ld a, SND_FM_TL_MAX`, `and SND_PSG_ATTEN_SILENT`, `ld b, PSGVOLENV_COUNT`, `set SCF_KEYED_B, (ix+d)` — ~25 sites). `map_plain_z80` (eval/asm.rs) short-circuited a single-segment non-register bareword straight to a link `Sym` (→ the symbolic-imm16-only path → `[lower.z80-unsupported]`), NEVER attempting the comptime fold that every COMPOUND expr (`CONST+0`, the `(ix+(field+1))` disp) already reaches. FIX: before falling to `Sym`, try `z80_bareword_const` (mirrors eval_path's const precedence: local binding → file const/equ → `-D` define, WITHOUT the label/unknown fallback) → fold to `CodeOperand::Imm`; a word that is NOT a comptime int stays a `Sym` (labels/call/jp targets unchanged). Matches the documented intent (asm.rs:1278 "a bare int / #n ... the Z80 lowering picks imm8/imm16 by form"). Z80-only, 68k path untouched. Tests: `z80_operands::z80_bare_const_folds_in_imm8_positions` / `z80_bare_const_bit_number_folds` / `z80_bare_label_still_symbol` + the psg byte gate (both shapes). Suite 2757→2765, frozen-68k held. — SHIPPED.
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **Z80 `(ix + field + k)` compound displacement flat-form parses `ix` as a name (parser precedence gap) — worked around by parenthesizing** — a STORE/READ `(ix+sc_base_freq+1)` (the 16-bit-field high-byte offset) errors `unknown name ix` (the flat `ix+expr` is evaluated as one arithmetic expression, losing the index-register recognition), whereas `(ix+sc_base_freq)` (single symbol) and `(ix+(sc_base_freq+1))` (PARENTHESIZED disp) both lower correctly and byte-identically. Sibling of the leftmost-local-label precedence sharp-edge (row 1610). WORKAROUND (shipped, byte-identical): parenthesize the compound displacement — `(ix+(field+1))`. THE ASK: the operand parser should bind a leading index register before the displacement arithmetic, so the natural flat form `(ix+field+1)` lowers. LEDGERED; the parenthesized spelling is the standing Z80 house form until fixed. — RECORDED (workaround shipped; parser fix deferred).
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **STOP-finding (checkpoint a): `clobbers()`/`out(<reg>)` with Z80 register names are NOT WIRED — validated against the 68k register file** — the rung-2 acceptance corpus surfaced that `check_clobbers` (`reglist_expand_checked`) and `check_out` (`reglist_set_quiet`) validate the reglist against the 68k universe (`d0-d7/a0-a7/sp`), so EVERY `clobbers(af)`/`clobbers(bc)`/`out(a)`/`out(hl)` on a Z80 proc errors `[proc.clobber-invalid]`/`[proc.out-invalid]` ("`af` is not a register (d0-d7/a0-a7/sp) or sr"). psg's full contract set fired 38 clobber-invalid + 5 out-invalid. Only `preserves(...)` uses the CPU-aware `expand_reglist(RegFile::Z80)` (item 1's recognizer); clobbers/out were never plumbed to it. The rung-2 note §2/§2.2 DESIGNED these to ride the CPU-aware recognizer, but the implementation (items 1-8) wired only preserves + invariant + out(carry:) — the `out(carry: found)` FLAG result works, but the `out(<reg>)` register result does not. THE FIX: thread the section/module CPU into `check_clobbers`/`check_out`'s reglist validation (route through `expand_reglist(RegFile::from(cpu))`), under the frozen-68k bar. psg LANDS with clobbers/out(reg) as PROSE (the .emp comments) pending this. — **CLOSED (finisher pass, sigil `d1830ee` gap 1; §13.5-A).** `check_clobbers`/`check_out` gained a `cpu` param and route a Z80 proc's reglist through `expand_reglist(RegFile::Z80)`; the out∩clobbers/out∩preserves overlap checks are Z80-expanded, and the out-UNWRITTEN check is SKIPPED on Z80 (its write detector is the 68k heuristic — a Z80 `out` is unverifiable-written, honest like `preserves`; a latent false-fire the prose contracts never reached). All 38 clobber-invalid + 5 out-invalid cleared; `clobbers`/`out(<reg>)` (incl. the `af`→`out(a) clobbers(f)` split) machine-checked on all 15 procs. Frozen-68k held (`preserves.rs` byte-untouched).
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **STOP-finding (checkpoint a): `z80_preserves` call-clobber-all blocks `invariant(ix)` + every calling proc's `preserves` — the Z80 analog of the t30 68k `verify_preserved` blocker, never given callee-awareness** — the rung-2 SIBLING proof (`z80_preserves.rs`) treats every `call` as clobber-all (line 315, `st.entry=[false;NU]`; `has_call → ever_clobbered=[true;NU]`), with NO callee-contract awareness. On psg (dense with `call Sub`), this false-fires `[proc.preserves-unverifiable]` on the 5 calling procs that RETURN after a call (Psg_ChBase / Psg_NoteOff / Psg_ApplyMod / Psg_EmitDivisor / Psg_SetVolume): 18 preserves + 5 `invariant(ix)` breaks — even though ix is only ever read as `(ix+d)` and the callees genuinely preserve. The design §3.2 "invariant(ix) trivially satisfied (no instruction writes ix)" is CONTRADICTED by its own acceptance corpus (the tests only ever exercised the NO-call case; a `call` writes-all in the model). NOTE the parallel: t30 hit the IDENTICAL wall for 68k (`verify_preserved` local clobber-all through a preserving call before rts) and FIXED it with the callee-preserves oracle (aeon merge b381a83 lineage) — but the Z80 sibling was built call-clobber-all (§13.3: "no transitive closure") and never got the equivalent. Also: procs that TAIL-JUMP out without a local `ret` (Psg_NoteOn / Psg_EmitNoiseClock / Psg_Noise) pass preserves VACUOUSLY (`!saw_return` → Verified) — a soundness SILENT-PASS (a tail-jp proc that DID write ix would wrongly verify). psg LANDS with `invariant(ix)` OMITTED and calling-proc `preserves` as PROSE; only the 7 leaf/no-call procs carry machine-checked `preserves`. THE FIX: give z80_preserves the same callee-declared-preserves threading the 68k oracle got (a call to `preserves(rN)` does not clobber rN), + close the vacuous tail-jp silent-pass. — **CLOSED (finisher pass, sigil `d1830ee` gaps 2+3; §13.5-B/C).** Gap 2: a `CalleePreserves` map (visible proc / `extern proc` → preserved units, the Z80 analog of `preserves.rs`'s `CallPolicy::Oracle`) — the `transfer` call arm now clobbers only the units the callee does NOT preserve; a local proc's map entry is its declared `preserves` ∪ the module invariant, an `extern`'s is its declared `preserves` (trusted). Gap 3: `Edge::Defer` (the tail-jp exit) now CHECKPOINTS and sets `saw_return`, so a tail-jp `preserves` is credited only if rN holds at the jp AND the tail-callee preserves it (permanent reproducer `z80_tail_jp_clobber_fires`). Follow-up self-catch (sigil `7441795`, §13.5-D): `Cfg::is_local_label` routes a jump to a LOCAL end-label (e.g. Psg_ApplyMod's `jr z, .div_ok` closing the proc before the `falls_into`) to `Edge::Abandon` (in-proc fall-off), not the external-tail `Edge::Defer` that had false-fired the inherited `invariant(ix)`. All 23 preserves-unverifiable cleared; `invariant(ix)` LIVE + inherited on all 15 procs (63→0). See the two residue rows below (declaration-trust w/o closure; out(carry) cross-proc unexercised).
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **header inaccuracy caught: `Psg_EnvCursorReset` .asm header `Clobbers af` over-claims — it clobbers NOTHING** — the routine is two `ld (ix+d), 0` immediate-to-memory stores (opcode DD 36, no register/flag effect) + `ret`, so it preserves EVERY register incl. af. The 15-year-old `Clobbers: af` header is over-broad. The `.emp` now carries `preserves(af, bc, de, hl)` — the checker VERIFIES the `af` the header wrongly claimed clobbered (the positive catch, machine-checked after the finisher's gap-1 wiring). The acceptance corpus caught THREE header over-claims total, all now machine-checked: (1) Psg_EnvCursorReset `Clobbers af` → clobbers nothing; (2) Psg/FmVolEnv_Resolve `Clobbers bc` → only `b` (the djnz counter) dies, `c` survives (`preserves(c)`); (3) Psg_EmitDivisor `Clobbers af, bc, de` → `de` (push/pop-bracketed) and `b` survive (`clobbers(a,c,f) preserves(b,de,hl)`). The exact "false clobber comment" class the psg file header warns about — the checker closes it. — RECORDED (all three now machine-verified; the acceptance corpus's header-accuracy dividend).

### t32 psg port — checkpoint (a) findings RESOLVED (finisher pass) + residue, 2026-07-29
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **RESIDUE (13.5-B): the Z80 callee-preserves oracle trusts DECLARATIONS per-proc, without a verified `effective` fixpoint — a cyclic mutual-preserve would be trusted** — the per-proc lowering seam has no transitive closure (unlike the 68k corpus closure's verified `effective`), so `CalleePreserves` credits a callee's DECLARED `preserves` directly. Sound for psg: own-writes are ALWAYS caught (a caller's own write clears its bit regardless of callee credit), each local proc is itself checked (the build stops at the root if a declared preserve is false), and psg is ACYCLIC. A genuinely cyclic mutual-preserve (`A preserves(x)` calls `B preserves(x)` calls `A`) would be trusted where the 68k closure would fold it conservative. No such cycle exists in psg/fm; named for a future rung if a Z80 preserve-cycle appears. — RECORDED (honest divergence from the 68k closure; not a psg blocker).
- [tranche 32 psg port, 2026-07-29] **RESIDUE: `out(carry: found)` on Psg/FmVolEnv_Resolve is UNEXERCISED cross-proc within psg — the consumers are external (sound_sequencer.asm)** — the flag-result machinery's caller-side must-consume check (`[call.flag-result-unused]`) is cross-proc, but the four callers of Psg/FmVolEnv_Resolve that `jr c`/`jr z` on the carry live in sound_sequencer.asm (`:674/:704/:744/:781`), OUTSIDE the psg module. The `out(carry: found)` DECLARATION + validity are machine-checked in psg (and feed the oracle), but the cross-proc CONSUMPTION credit only exercises when the sequencer is ported (rung 3) into the same corpus. Until then no psg-local caller `jr c`s on it. Named so the sequencer port re-checks the flag-result cross-proc credit end-to-end. — RECORDED (out(carry:) wired + declared; cross-proc consumption awaits the sequencer port).

- [tranche 33 fm port, 2026-07-29] **DEMAND 1 (f-tracking, overseer ruling 2): `z80_writes` never marks the `f` unit, so `preserves(f)` is unverifiable (manual-honor only)** — the sibling proof's write set (`z80_preserves.rs:118 z80_writes`) returns NO flag unit for ANY instruction (add/sub/cp/bit etc. write `a`/dest but never `f`); `f` is cleared only by a `call`/external-tail (clobber-all) or a foreign `pop af`. So a lone `preserves(f)` on a flag-clobbering proc would VERIFY vacuously (the checker cannot catch the lie), and a genuinely-f-preserving proc's `preserves(f)` is a claim on the porter's honor. ### ambient-hoist parcel — engine.constants hoist + BUTTON adoption, 2026-07-29
- [ambient-hoist parcel, 2026-07-29] **engine.constants hoist DONE (kill rows 76/77/81 reduced) + the CORRECTION on PPHYS_*/BUTTON_JUMP_MASK.** The engine-truth `const`+`ensure` mirrors of `engine/constants.asm` — 45 symbols: 26 `PHYS_*` (Sonic's 8 PhysTable values + player_common's SKID_MIN/JUMP_BUFFER/FALL_CAP + player_ground's 15 slope/roll/slip/gsp/headroom/move-lock), 6 radii/CURL/PUSH, 3 `ST_XFLIP`/`ST_ROLLING`/`ST_PUSHING`, `COLLISION_NONE`, 3 `EDGE_*`, `BUTTON_A`/`BUTTON_C`, 4 `BUTTON_*_BIT` — HOISTED out of `player_common.emp`/`sonic.emp`/`player_{ground,air,spindash}.emp` into the shared `engine.constants` twin (`engine/system/constants.emp`), consumed via `use engine.constants.{...}`; the drift guards MOVED with the consts (no guard dropped). Test-ambient: the 45 pairs added to `test_support::engine_constant_equs()` (now 115; the self-count test renamed `_all_70`→`_all_115`), and the now-duplicate engine-truth pushes REMOVED from `test_p1_player_port.rs` + `test_p2_player_states_port.rs` (else the equ blob double-defines). All 45 are defined in `engine/constants.asm`, so every constants-consuming mixed build (which already assembles that file for SECTION_SIZE etc.) resolves them for free. **CORRECTION to the brief/rows-76/77/81:** `PPHYS_*` (physics-table field offsets) and `BUTTON_JUMP_MASK` are GAME-truth — defined in `player_common.asm`, NOT `engine/constants.asm` (verified: `PPHYS_ACCEL = Phys_accel-Player_Phys`, `BUTTON_JUMP_MASK = BUTTON_A|BUTTON_C`). Hoisting them into `engine.constants` would (a) invert engine→game layering and (b) force every constants-consuming mixed build (controllers/collision/rings/…, none of which assemble player_common.asm) to link it. So they stay file-local — same class as the game-config mirrors (PSTATE_*/SFXID_*) the brief keeps. Their natural home when a game-constants `.emp` module is born is `player_common.emp` (`pub const`), not the engine twin. — CLOSED (engine block hoisted; game-truth remainder correctly stays; rows 76/77/81 reduced to their game-config/PPHYS remainder).
- [ambient-hoist parcel, 2026-07-29] **BUTTON_*_BIT adoption DONE (the t35 item-13 A1 wait discharged).** With `BUTTON_UP/DOWN/LEFT/RIGHT_BIT` now ambient from `engine.constants`, the ported player files' commented raw `btst #N` d-pad sites adopted the named consts (byte-neutral folds; bare `// LEFT`/`// RIGHT`/`// UP`/`// DOWN` comments died, semantic tails kept): player_common `Player_DebugMove` ×4, player_air ×3 (air-input L/R + Air_LandState DOWN), player_ground ×6 (spindash-gate DOWN, PostMove DOWN, roll-brake L/R ×4, move L/R), player_spindash ×1 (DOWN). `player_ground.emp:265 btst #0, Player_Quadrant` (a quadrant bit, not a button) and `player_common.emp:333 btst #4, d6 // BUTTON_B` (no `BUTTON_B_BIT` const exists) correctly LEFT raw. The `ButtonMask`/`ButtonBit` newtype split (t35 item-13, ledger 1787/1603) is a SEPARATE arithmetic-preservation-gated design call, NOT touched here. — CLOSED (adoption; newtype still ledgered).

### t35 abs_w step-6 corpus sweep, 2026-07-29
- [t35 step-6 sweep, 2026-07-29] **abs_w ENGINE-HOIST + 6 engine folds — BLOCKED-LEDGERED (engine→game layering + large test-ambient blast radius).** The mandatory step-6 sweep for kill-row-82's abs_w retrofitted player_common.emp ×3 (`.abs_ok`/`.skid_abs`/`.wr_abs`, done, byte-neutral). It ALSO found SIX further genuine-idiom `bpl/neg.w` abs sites (each CC-liveness verified — the bpl reads the sign of the register neg'd, set by the immediately-preceding move.w/sub.w; labels 2-ref only): **entity_window.emp** (`.sx_pos` d1, `.sy_pos` d0 — |slot−center|), **collision.emp** (`.solid_ax_pos` d1, `.solid_ay_pos` d3 — AABB |delta|), **core.emp** (`.culled_xpos` d0, `.culled_ypos` d0 — |pos−camera| cull). The clean fix is an abs_w engine-hoist (natural home `engine.coords`, the leaf comptime-arithmetic module beside `pixels_to_coord`) — but that has a LARGE test-ambient blast radius: every test/mixed-build that compiles core/collision/entity_window must gain a coords ambient (verified by attempt: the 3 `*_port.rs` + `core_negative_probes.rs` + `tranche7_negative_probes.rs` + the mixed-build `placed_module_sections` collision arm + likely the core/entity_window mixed arms). LESSON: abs_w was step-4-placed in player_common (game) because the panel's immediate consumers were the player files; the corpus sweep REVEALED it is a corpus-wide engine idiom — the "first-consumer designs the interface" wrong-home case. — LEDGERED (byte-neutral engine-hoist parcel; the 6 folds pend on it; kill-row 82 tracks). — **CLOSED 2026-07-29 (the ambient-hoist parcel, sigil branch `hoist-ambient`).** `abs_w` RE-HOMED `player_common.emp` → `engine.coords.emp` (beside `pixels_to_coord`); all consumers (player_ground/air ×9, player_common ×3, + the 6 engine folds) `use engine.coords.{abs_w}`. The 6 engine folds LANDED byte-neutral (entity_window `Collected_UpdateCenter` ×2, collision `Touch_Solid` ×2, core `RunObjects` ×2). Test-ambient additions (re-derived, NOT trusted from the estimate above — the estimate MISSED entity_window's two-module-flip path and tranche7's `lower_quintet` helper): coords ambient added to `entity_window_port.rs` (BOTH compile paths — the region gate + the section↔entity_window two-module flip), `collision_port.rs`, `core_port.rs`, `core_negative_probes.rs`, `tranche7_negative_probes.rs` (Sources + `lower_quintet` 6th param), and `mixed_dac_rom.rs` (collision.emp arm). core.emp/entity_window.emp are NOT in any mixed build (no mixed change). ### t37 sound_sfx step-1, 2026-07-29
- [t37 sfx step-1, 2026-07-29] **transitive clobbers-completeness diagnostic — DEMANDED-DIAGNOSTIC (the analog of the extern-decl-vs-def row).** The `[proc.clobbers-undeclared]` checker enforces clobbers-completeness LOCALLY only: a proc's declared `clobbers` must cover its OWN body's register writes, but NOT the callee-union its `call`s destroy. PROVEN at t37: `Sfx_Frame`'s `.asm` header declares `clobbers(af,bc,de,hl,ix)` and OMITS `iy`, yet its callees Sfx_DrainQueue / Sfx_DuckRamp / Sfx_Restore destroy iy (their iy-walkers) — removing iy from the `.emp` Sfx_Frame's clobbers does NOT fire the checker (empirically verified, reverted). So a clobbers UNDER-claim across calls is silently uncaught, exactly as a stale `extern proc` preserves silently drifts (t36 row). This is unsafe: an omitted clobber falsely tells callers the register is PRESERVED (the exhaustive-license semantics). The `.emp` closes it BY DISCIPLINE (declare the honest superset = local ∪ callee-union), but the checker should verify it: a `[call.clobbers-incomplete]` lint = "declared clobbers must be a superset of every reachable callee's clobbers ∪ local writes" (the same fixpoint the honest-contract closure rule already specifies in prose). THE LIVE INSTANCE: Sfx_Frame — the FIRST clobbers under-claim caught in the sound corpus (psg/fm/sequencer had only over-claims). The `.asm` header comment is fixed in lockstep (comment-only, byte-neutral, gate-proven). KILL = the diagnostic landing OR the seam sub-tranche (whichever first — same kill as the extern-decl-vs-def row). — LEDGERED (demanded diagnostic; overseer-directed at checkpoint (a)).
- [t37 sfx step-2 type-layer, 2026-07-29] **sound_sfx Z80-side domain-type candidates — LEDGERED, not adopted (compute-heavy + newtype-absent).** The step-2 type-layer walk over sound_sfx.emp finds five domain values that two same-width bytes could swap: raw SFX id (`a`, range-checked then table-indexed), priority (`SFXPRI_*`, compare-arbitrated), physical route (`CHROUTE_*`), voice kind (`SFXEL_*`), and slot index (0..6). NONE adopted: (1) the newtypes do not exist as Z80-usable types — `SfxId` is the ledgered T1 item-13 candidate but const-born at the 68k `SFX_*` defs and flows the 68k SOUND-API, not the Z80 interpreter internals (gap-ledger MusicId/SfxId row); (2) every one lives in COMPUTE-HEAVY chains — the id in a range-subtract, routes/kinds/slots in table-index arithmetic (`add a, l` into SfxEligTable/SfxRouteSlot/SfxSlotRoute), priorities in `cp` compare chains — the value-flow test defers shift/add-chain values to arithmetic-preservation (A4-i); (3) STOP-not-absorb holds (byte movement ZERO — a type layer is byte-neutral, but building the Z80 newtype family is a step-4 ASK, not a step-2 adopt). KILL = the A4-i arithmetic-preservation landing + a Z80-side newtype family (Route/Kind/Slot/SfxPriority), or the seam sub-tranche folding sfx into the linked sound module where the 68k SfxId boundary reaches it. — LEDGERED (step-2 item-6 not-adopted, reason logged per the rule).
- [t37 sfx step-3/4, 2026-07-29] **sound_sfx CONSTRUCT-adoption candidates — LEDGERED byte-neutral (byte-frozen tranche, t36-parallel STOP).** Step-3(a) ceremony scan + step-4: (1) **`table_base_plus_idx` / the `ix_field_ptr` FAMILY — 8 sfx-side sites** (all in Sfx_SelectVoice's 3-tier ladder): the `ld hl, TABLE; ld a, idx; add a, l; ld l, a; ld a, 0; adc a, h; ld h, a` 8-bit-index-into-16-bit-base-with-carry idiom (7 lines for `hl = &TABLE[idx]`, high lines-per-intent). This is the LABEL-base sibling of the t36-B1 ledgered `ix_field_ptr` (row 1781; ix-base `push ix/pop rr` + field-add-with-carry) — a generalized `base_plus_idx(base, idx)` comptime-fn would cover BOTH (t36's 3 sequencer + 1 fm sites + these 8 = 12 cross-corpus sites, the demand data the build case wants). sfx has ZERO true-ix sites (it uses `(ix+field)` direct addressing), so its 8 are the label-base variant. (2) The `push ix; push iy; pop ix` register-move-via-stack idiom (ix<->iy swap around music-channel work) recurs but is Z80-native and clean — NOT a construct candidate. NEITHER built in this byte-frozen checkpoint-(b) STOP (matches t36's build-nothing-in-tranche outcome) → the step-6 corpus sweep / seam sub-tranche / overseer gate rules build-vs-ledger. KILL = the `base_plus_idx` construct landing or the seam sub-tranche. — LEDGERED (adds 8 sites to the ix_field_ptr row-1781 cross-file build case).

### tranche 38 P4 player_sensors — verifier finding + corrections, 2026-07-29
- [t38 P4, 2026-07-29] **VERIFIER GAP (overseer-ACCEPTED, sharpened kill): the shared CFG mis-classifies `bsr` as a CONDITIONAL BRANCH, not a call.** `flag_check::Cfg::edges()` matches `bsr` under `mnem.starts_with('b') && mnem.len() == 3`, so a `bsr.s .local` flows INTO the local subroutine (a `Follow` edge to the target) AND treats that subroutine's `rts` as the ENCLOSING proc's return. `jsr`/`jbsr` do NOT match the pattern → correctly modeled as calls (`Follow`-next-only). `bsr` and `jbsr` are the SAME instruction (`CALL_MNEMONICS` lists all three), so this is a latent classifier LIE. It surfaced at t38 as the SECOND root cause of the probe-core a0 firings (the overseer's sp-cleanup ruling named the first): `bsr.s .cell` into probe_core's `.cell` subroutine made its `rts` a proc-return where a0 is clobbered (Collision_GetType) and unrestored → refuted `preserves(a0)`. RESOLVED byte-neutrally by the `bsr.s .cell`→`jbsr .cell` house-format conversion (short reach → jbsr picks bsr.s), which removes ALL corpus exposure (post-step-2 no `bsr` remains in `.emp`). So the gap is **latent-ZERO-exposure** — but the classifier lie stays live until touched. **KILL (overseer-sharpened 2026-07-29): the NEXT PARCEL THAT ALREADY TOUCHES the shared CFG (`flag_check.rs`) OR the flag/preserves analyses fixes the classifier — model `CALL_MNEMONICS` (jsr/jbsr/`bsr`) uniformly as calls (one-line `edges()` change + the blast-radius test sweep across flag_check/preserves/z80).** NOT fixed at t38 (a shared-CFG change carries flag_check blast radius; the jbsr conversion is the lower-risk, campaign-aligned resolution and the overseer ruled the conversion sufficient for exposure). A FOURTH-face cousin of the drift diagnostic, verifier-side (Z80 silent-tolerate / Z80 local-pass / 68k refuse-outright / 68k verifier-precision-limit / **this: 68k CFG-classifier lie**). — **CLOSED** (cfg-edges-unification, 2026-08-06): the kill condition fired — this parcel touches the shared CFG (`flag_check.rs`), and the row's demand (model `bsr` as a call, not a conditional branch, in `edges()`) was already satisfied by B′-2's `!= "bsr"` exclusion (row 2108, CLOSED): `bsr` falls to the plain-instruction arm (`Follow`-next-only), uniform with `jsr`/`jbsr`, pinned by `a_bsr_calls_it_does_not_branch` + `no_call_mnemonic_yields_a_transfer_out_edge`. The row asked ONLY for the `edges()` fix, which shipped; the more ambitious ISA-crate `is_call`/`is_return`/`is_branch` classifier is a SEPARATE demand (row 2150) and stays OPEN.

Fixing it INSIDE T1 would spend a byte-changing hot-path parallax wave + oracle A/B on a behavior no shipped content exercises, inside the tranche whose whole point is closing the game side cleanly — so the fix gets its OWN vehicle. **THE PARCEL:** a per-frame (or on-active-change) mode-3 write in `Parallax_Update` derived from `Parallax_Active_Config` (which already returns the active config in d0/Z), computing the same mode bits `Parallax_StartTransition.update_mode` computes, then the harness force-write DIES (a one-block deletion in `ojz_scroll_test.emp` + its `.asm` twin, its own byte-changing mini-wave). **STEP-0 SUB-QUESTION (named):** whether the engine also needs the DIRECT reg-$0B write (the ojz harness distrusts the shadow flush here — a `VDP_CTRL` read to reset the command state machine against a half-finished 32-bit address command from `Section_UpdateColumns`), or can rely on the normal VBlank shadow→reg flush; an engine write touching only the shadow is a behavioral change from the harness's direct hardware write. **COST CLASS:** byte-CHANGING edit on the already-merged `parallax.emp` + its lockstep twin `parallax.asm` (kill row 5) + re-pin the PARALLAX region (kill row 6); per-frame HOT PATH → step-5 C1 (cycle) ACTIVE + C3 (VDP/timing) ACTIVE + the **oracle A/B** rider with TWO named checks in its A/B plan: (a) the engine now writes the same mode the harness did (no render regression on the ojz boot scene), and (b) the extra per-frame engine write does NOT perturb the deterministic `Debug_Scene_Freeze` cache-fill soak ojz drives (a live oracle instrument). This is the parcel-scope-amendment class (a standalone hardening parcel on an already-ported file, deferrable to the post-conversion optimization sweep, ~half-cost after asl retires: one source file, no twin lockstep, no re-pin). — LEDGERED (standalone parcel; row-35 kill clause updated; the fix-now-vs-later promotion surfaced to Volence with carry-as-is as the executed default — if promoted it runs after t41 regardless, nothing foreclosed).

- [seam-1 FINISHER dry-panel B1, 2026-07-29] **DRY defect: the Z80 inter-proc-edge detector has THREE divergent copies — ledgered, kill = the next `z80_preserves` CFG toucher unifies them.** The seam-1 diagnostic's `transfer_target` (sigil `seam1.rs` — the `call`/`rst`/`jp`/`jr`/`djnz` Sym-target extractor with the leading-Cc skip + `.`-local / `$`-hygiene filter) re-implements the SAME "is this a real Z80 inter-proc edge?" predicate that already exists TWICE in `z80_preserves.rs`: `branch_sym` (the branch-target extractor) + the preserves proof's local-label test (`cfg.is_local_label` / `cfg.label_index`). ### flip Stage 1 S1.4 — the sigil-canonical deb2 appendix (Option A), 2026-07-30
- [S1.4, 2026-07-30] **APPENDIX NAME-QUALITY POLISH — a listing-side demangler; sharpened: convsym DROPS the `$`-mangled names outright (not merely ugly).** Under Option A the deb2 appendix is sigil-canonical (the `.emp` names are the source names going forward, asl leaves at Stage 2). The measurement (2026-07-30-flip-stage1-S1.4-appendix-fork.md): sigil's joint link table has 1585 labels / 1394 in convsym range, but `tools/convsym`'s `as_lst` reader **KEEPS ONLY 676** — every `$`-mangled name (906 of them: inline-`asm{}`-block locals `$mod$asmN$local`, comptime-table labels `__offsets$mod$table$entry`, `__align$…`) is silently DROPPED by convsym's name parser (`$` is not a legal as-listing symbol char). So the synthetic names aren't just a debugger-UX regression vs asl's flat `Ani_Sonic_Balance` — they never reach the debugger at all. THE FIX (post-flip, ruling-endorsed): a listing-side demangler in `sigil-link::listing`/`native::build_native_rom_with_listing` that rewrites `$module$Parent$local` → `Parent.local` (and picks a policy for the `asmN`/`__offsets` classes, see row (ii)) BEFORE `emit_listing`, so the ~906 locals SURVIVE convsym and the appendix grows from 676 → ~1394 symbols. Byte-neutral to the ROM (listing-only), and it re-bases the sigil-canonical full-file golden (a one-step re-freeze). Does NOT block Stage 1/2 — the 676 kept symbols are every named global proc (AnimateSprite, BG_Init, GameLoop, TouchResponse, every player/level/sound entry), a serviceable load-bearing table. KILL: build the demangler in the post-flip §17 appendix-polish parcel; re-freeze the six goldens. — LEDGERED (post-flip; sharpened with the convsym-drop fact; blocks nothing).
- [S1.4, 2026-07-30] **APPENDIX-CONTENT AUDIT — signal-vs-noise on sigil's extra/synthetic locals.** Sigil's listing carries ~50–72 genuinely-extra code labels asl never emitted (`Process_DMA_Critical.drain_1..8`, `asmN.wait_z80`, `TestArt_Ring`, `EmitterPositions`) plus the 906 inline-asm/comptime synthetic locals (row (i)). Once the demangler lets the synthetic locals through convsym, the appendix would carry inline-asm-block micro-locals (`asm2.clamp`, `asmN.wait_z80`) that are arguably NOISE in a debugger backtrace (asl's own list had the same `asmN`-class noise; the overseer flagged they "may deserve filtering"). THE AUDIT (post-flip, pairs with row (i)): decide a policy — emit all locals (max fidelity), demangle-and-keep only `Parent.local` non-`asm` locals (drop `asmN.*` block-internal churn), or a name-prefix filter — then bake it into the demangler. This is a UX/policy call (what belongs in the shipped debug symbol table), deliberately deferred so the demangler and the filter policy land together. Does NOT block Stage 1/2. KILL: settle the policy in the §17 appendix-polish parcel alongside row (i). — LEDGERED (post-flip; UX/policy call; blocks nothing).

### conv-h2 embed construct + boot_data STOP + Parcel J, 2026-08-01
- [conv-h2, 2026-08-01] **The conv-h "`.emp` has NO binary-embed" row is now HALF-RESOLVED — `embed(...)` SHIPPED.** The `embed("root/rel/path.bin")` `.emp` data expression (spec 2026-08-01, `eval_embed`) inlines an external binary as `Value::Data`, so BootData's resident-Z80-blob `BINCLUDE` HAS an `.emp` spelling now. What still blocks #12 boot_data is NOT the blob — it is the **conditional `org $3FE` HOLE** the no-sound arm leaves for the separately-chained `z80_init.emp` idle program. `.emp` has `align`/byte-count `pad` but NO absolute-`org`/reserved-hole surface; a `pad` EMITS bytes and would collide with the chainer-placed `z80_init` at `$3D8`. The hole is LIVE for demo/demo.debug (2 of 6 targets). Unblocking #12 needs either an `.emp` absolute-org/reserved-hole data surface OR a chainer inter-module-hole mechanism (place module B into a gap module A leaves), plus a conditional two-module boot_data split. STOPPED per embed spec §3. — LEDGERED (the TRUE #12 prerequisite; supersedes the conv-h binary-embed row for the blob half). — **RESOLVED (K2, 2026-08-01): NO new surface was needed.** The insight: the $3FE "hole" is not an empty gap needing a reserve-surface — it is where the separately-chained `z80_init` idle physically LANDS (head|idle|tail is contiguous in the final ROM). So the two-module split (`boot_head` + `boot_tail`, K2 landed exactly that) plus the existing CONTIGUOUS declared-order packer places `boot_head < Z80_IdleProgram < boot_tail` with no gaps — the same mechanism z80_init's own port used. The only real requirement was a `BootData_End` frozen boundary key (an `.emp` section carries no baked lma, so the tail needs the frozen pin the AS residual got for free). `embed(...)` supplies the blob half. Six-target byte-clean (header-neutral anchor identical ×6). The "absolute-org/reserved-hole surface" and "inter-module-hole mechanism" are NOT needed for boot_data (and no other consumer wants them) — this row closes. See `2026-08-01-k2-boot-data.md`.
- [conv-h2, 2026-08-01] **`[u8; _]` inferred-length spelling is not wired** — `_` in array-length position parses as an ordinary path (not an inference hole) in `layout.rs::resolve_type`. Inference works by OMITTING the annotation entirely (`pub data X = embed(...)`), so the `_` sugar is cosmetic; the embed spec's §1 contingency already blesses omit-to-infer as the v1 whole. If `_`-as-hole is wanted for readability parity with the spec text, add an `Expr::Wildcard`/`Type::Array` inference path that sizes from the initializer's `Value::Data` length. — LEDGERED (cosmetic language nicety; low priority). **DECLINED (language round, 2026-08-02, L6 — specs/2026-08-02-language-round-agenda.md Tier 3): the omit-annotation form (`pub data X = embed(...)`) is the blessed v1 idiom (embed spec §1); `_` would be a second spelling for the same thing — one way to do it.**

### conv-i8 — the #8 compression-vectors generator port, 2026-08-01
- [conv-i8, 2026-08-01] **`.emp` has no size-relaxation-tolerant `align` after code, nor a per-data-item alignment attribute.** Folding the five golden-vector `embed()`s into `compression_selftest.emp` (after the `CompressionSelfTest` proc) tried to mirror the AS twin's inter-blob `align 2`, but a top-level `align 2` sitting after a section whose proc holds size-relaxable branches (`bra.s`/`beq.s`) fires `[align.provisional] alignment pad at a provisional position`. The align can't resolve because the proc's final size isn't pinned until relaxation converges — a chicken-and-egg the assembler stops on rather than iterating. WORKAROUND (shipped): every golden blob is even-length today, so the labels word-align with NO padding; the aligns were dropped and five comptime `ensure(_Vec_X.len % 2 == 0, …)` guards added (fire loudly if a future encoding makes a blob odd, pointing at the fix). The real fix is either (a) an `align` that participates in the relaxation fixpoint (re-evaluate pad position after branch sizes settle), or (b) a per-`data`-item alignment attribute (`pub data X: align(2) = embed(...)`) that pins the item's own base independent of what precedes it. Low priority (the even-length invariant + guards cover it), but any port folding data after relaxable code in one section will hit this. — LEDGERED (language: relaxation-aware align / per-item align attribute).

Machinery: registry ModuleSpecs + pins ENTITY_DATA/OJZ_ACT_POOL + repin.toml regions (no gate) + frozen key OJZ_Act_Pool_Page0 ×4 tables (OJZ_Sec0_TypeTable existed) + map-order row + golden-gate `ojz_run_a_port` (entity_data with an ObjDef seam; ojz_act_pool self-contained). ### K4 inc-5 Stage 1 — the B1 epilogue (EndOfRom native), 2026-08-01
- [K4 inc-5 S1, 2026-08-01] **`EndOfRom` + the 3 ROM-tail walls are native (`engine/system/epilogue.emp`, `module engine.epilogue`) — six-target FULL byte-identical, no re-freeze.** A zero-length `pub data EndOfRom = Data.empty` placed LAST (boundary key EndOfRom, already frozen ×6; packs abutting error_handler's tail). The walls split by nature: the plane-cells wall (pure constants) stays a comptime `ensure`; EndOfRom evenness + the 4 MB ceiling depend on the PLACED address so they are LINK-time asserts (`ensure(extern("EndOfRom") …)` → LinkAssert, the vdp/parallax ensure-over-extern precedent). engine.inc holes the AS `EndOfRom:` label + walls + the inert resume-org behind `ifndef SIGIL_EMP_EPILOGUE`. No map.toml change (EndOfRom is a zero-byte marker, excluded from order validation). repin 0-changed (67→68 regions, hand pin matched the tool); refreeze --check OK (chain 14 untouched); strict 2895/0/4. **GAP (step-5 hardening, not built): a negative probe for the three walls** — an odd/over-4MB EndOfRom should fire the two link asserts; a plane-cell over-count should fire the comptime ensure. Today the walls have only implicit build-path coverage (a malformed assert fails every build, so they are well-formed and true; but nothing proves they FIRE on a bad input). Not built because the terminus/plane constants are not easily doctored without a synthetic harness (the collision/error_handler negative-probe pattern would need a doctored-EndOfRom fixture). — LEDGERED (wall negative-probe; low priority; the six-target identity + the always-run asserts cover the live path).

### A1 — seam-2 registry unification: the emit reads the map, 2026-08-02
- [A1, 2026-08-02] **✅ CLOSED — the seam-2 dual placement registry (K-capstone §6 P1; language-round ledger SECTION 3 A1).** The emit tool was the SECOND registry of the same placement facts the map owns: `seam2.rs` hard-coded ~10 LMA consts (`DAC_BLIP_LMA` 0x48000 / `DAC_SHARED_LMA` 0x50000 / `SOUND_TABLES_Z80_LMA` 0x58000 / `PITCHTABLE_LMA` 0x58357 / `SFX_WIN_TAB_LMA` 0x5845F / `SEQ_OPCODE_TAB_LMA` 0x5856D / `DAC_SAMPLE_TAB_LMA` 0x585AD / `MT_BANK_LMA` 0x58607 / `SFX_BANK_LMA_{PLAIN,DEBUG}` 0x5BAE8/0x5D53A) that duplicated `games/sonic4/map.toml`'s two anchors — a placement move needed re-pinning both. ### A3 — the comptime emitted-span primitive: hand literals retired, 2026-08-02
- [A3, 2026-08-02] **✅ CLOSED — the comptime section-length / table-span primitive (rows 1654 / 1805 / 1911; language-round ledger SECTION 3 A3; K-capstone §6 P1's A1/A2 arc, third parcel).** Shipped as `span(ProcName)` — a single-segment, non-shadowable comptime builtin (the `here()` / `cycles()` family) returning the EMITTED byte span of a PURE-DATA proc body. It measures by re-using the real body lowering (`eval_asm_owned`, the same entry `lower::proc::eval_proc_body` runs, driving the identical `lower_dc`) and summing the resulting `CodeBuf`: `CodeItem::Inline` contributes its `DataBuf::size` (the per-cell byte sum — 2 for a `dc.w Label` `Cell::SymRef` even while the label is link-deferred), a label 0; an instruction in the body is `[span.not-data]` (a code proc's length is a link-time fact, out of the demand). The measurement is a pure query — `diags` / `dropped_instrs` snapshotted-and-restored so a malformed body is reported once by the actual emission, never doubled. **Two targets adopted, byte-identical ×6** (s4 0bb6ee97/412232 · s4.debug 76d06e43/422066 · demo c6954bd4/90649 · demo.debug 9c1e1b64/93145; config_a/config_b ride the golden gates): (1) `dac_sample_tab.emp:59`'s `10*9` LHS → `span(DacSampleTable)` — a MEASURED emitted span (90 B), so the guard now catches this body emitting the wrong NUMBER of descriptors, exactly what the deleted AS `(DacSampleTable_End - DacSampleTable) <> …` guard did and the literal could not; (2) `FMVOLENV_COUNT` / `PSGVOLENV_COUNT` become pub consts of `sound_tables_z80.emp` derived `span(*VolEnv_Ids)` (one `db` per env → the count), resolved AUTHORITY-FIRST by a new memoized `sound_tables_authority_consts` in `seam1.rs` — the two `seam_emit_config` literals DELETED, so the resident scan count can no longer drift from the emitted table. **Row 1879 (the id/ptr count-hardening deferral) is DISCHARGED as a bonus:** the deleted AS twin's `if (…_Ptrs_End - …_Ptrs) <> COUNT*2 / error` internal guard is REVIVED as a real in-section `ensure(span(*VolEnv_Ptrs) == span(*VolEnv_Ids) * 2)` (in-section, not top-level — a module-level ensure opens an unplaced default `text` section under the co-link placement; the pub consts stay module-level, they emit nothing). Both `.emp` edits flow through `tools/gen_sound_tables.py` (`emit_emp_z80`), the generated file regenerated. `pins.rs` / `repin_pins.rs` UNCHANGED (`repin --check` clean); NO re-freeze (chain 22, `refreeze --check` OK); strict 2990/0/4 (2983 + 7 span-primitive tests, incl. the two doctored-length negatives + the `[span.not-data]` scope wall + the missing-proc error). Design note: `docs/superpowers/notes/2026-08-02-a3-span-design.md`. **Still open (declined — out of the demand):** row 1910 the 6 vol-env CONTROL bytes (`*VolEnvCtl_Loop/Sustain/Rest`) remain hand emit-config; harvesting them touches only the generator, not this primitive. — DONE.

### Memory-safety design round (Volence + Fable conversation), 2026-08-03
- [design-round, 2026-08-03] **Effect/context tracking as proc attributes + bracket introduction form** — Volence-blessed direction. One feature, two faces: a `requires(...)` proc attribute (e.g. `requires(z80_stopped)`, `requires(vblank)`) propagated transitively up the call graph exactly like `clobbers`/`preserves` compose, plus a scoped bracket construct (`with_z80_stopped { }`) as the only introduction form — the bracket proves acquire/release pairing on every path, the attribute proves call-site context. Evidence: `engine/z80_bus.emp` enforces stop/start pairing with a doc comment only ("Pair with start_z80"); ~10 consumer files splice the pair on the honor system; a missed release starves the Z80 (sound hang). The 90% bracket is expressible TODAY as a higher-order comptime fn (`stop_z80() ++ body ++ start_z80()` + unexporting the raw pair); the language work that earns its keep is (a) the escape-path check — a branch/rts out of the body skips the release and a comptime fn cannot see into `Code` — and (b) the `requires` propagation. Rides the D2.32/D2.35 proc-attribute machinery. GROUNDING UPDATE (same conversation): this is not a new family — S2-D7(d) already spec'd the `stopZ80`/`startZ80` + `disableInts`/`enableInts` pairing lints (decided 2026-07-02, deferred on the S2-D6/D7 shared dataflow pass), and S2-D6(a) spec'd transitive contract propagation. Volence's holistic ask (2026-08-03): scope the spec as THE CONTRACT UNIFICATION — build the S2-D6/D7 dataflow pass with `requires`/bracket as a new facet of one per-proc contract record (registers/stack/CCR/contexts/cycles, one propagation engine, per-facet composition), not another syntactic slice. Surface spellings KEEP (D2.13 semantics-not-syntax; corpus-wide adoption); header growth answered by S2-D6(a) inference — derive contracts for internal procs, declared = checked assertions at pub boundaries + computed-jump barriers. L-scale. — OPEN (spec candidate for the language round; placement = Volence's call)
- [design-round, 2026-08-03] **Niche-sentinel Option** — Volence-blessed direction. Runtime `Option` via the sentinel-as-niche representation (Rust `NonZero` / Maybe-with-known-niche): a refined newtype whose excluded value ($FF = no slot, 0 = null object) IS the None tag, erasing to the same byte, with a forced match/branch extract so "forgot to test for $FF before indexing" becomes a compile error. Does NOT reverse D2.14's runtime-tagged-union rejection — the sentinel is the cheap representation D2.14 said didn't exist. Rides newtype + refinement (`where`) machinery; natural neighbor of the L5/L8 type-layer arc. — OPEN (spec candidate)
- [design-round, 2026-08-03] **Verify clobbers/preserves/out enforcement depth** — D2.32/D2.35 landed as the *syntactic* S2-D6 slices; before the effect-tracking spec builds on that machinery, confirm how much body/caller verification actually ships today vs. declaration-only. — **ANSWERED 2026-08-04 (overseer state audit, `notes/2026-08-04-finish-line-state-audit.md` §4): MUCH deeper than declaration-only — contract-grammar v2 ships a whole-corpus transitive closure (`closure.rs`/`corpus_contracts.rs`) over instruction-derived write sets, plus `[call.live-clobbered]`+`[call.input-undefined]` (CFG dataflow), `[bus.*]` (3-point lattice, path-sensitive Z80/VDP hazard net), `flag_check` (CCR), `out_verify` w/ CondOutMap. ### B'-0 era lens panel (A/B/C), 2026-08-04 — the conditional-out relax
- [bprime-0c, 2026-08-04] **NEW (opened by the item-2/3 fix): `subcontract_violations` compares conditional-out REGISTERS but not their condition CODES.** A target declaring `out(a1 if ne)` satisfies a bound declaring `out(a1 if eq)` — the register is produced on SOME edge, but not the one the bound's callers test. Register-level was the right first cut (the split had to exist before the cc could be compared, and no `interface`/`hook` binding exists in aeon yet), but the cc dimension is a real hole in the §4 relation, not a nit: the two contracts describe opposite edges. Fix: carry `(reg, cc)` pairs in `Contract::out_cond` and require the target's guard to be the bound's, or provably weaker. The `[contract.hook-signature]` message already names the register, so the diagnostic surface needs no change. — OPEN
- [bprime-0 lens C, 2026-08-04] **THE RELAX OPENED A SILENT-LIE SURFACE — the parcel's own cost, escalated to Fable as a B' spec item.** `[proc.out-clobbers-overlap]` was the ONLY thing forcing `out(rN if cc)` to have one meaning. After B'-0 the corpus carries TWO incompatible readings of the same syntax, distinguished solely by whether the author ALSO typed the register into `clobbers` — and NO checker verifies which is true: `AllocDynamic clobbers(d0/a1) out(a1 if eq)` means "a1 destroyed on every edge"; `AllocEffect clobbers(d0) out(a1 if eq)` means "a1 survives the ¬eq edge" (true today — its pool test precedes its pop and it has no latch path). §9 `check_firings` accepts both because `allowed = clobbers ∪ out` and a1 ∈ out either way. **Concrete regression that now compiles CLEAN with zero diagnostics:** hoist `AllocEffect`'s pop above its exhaustion test — a1 is then trashed on the failure edge while the contract still says `clobbers(d0)`; D1c is unaffected (it reads body-derived `effective`), and nothing compares the clobbers/out split against the CFG. `children.emp:578`'s exhaustive-license reasoning would be silently invalidated. **BEFORE the parcel this was unexpressible** — the split could not encode a false claim because the grammar rejected it; the CONTRACT CAVEAT prose carried the fact instead. FIX CANDIDATES (Fable rules): (a) require a cond-out register listed in `clobbers` to be PROVEN written on some ¬cc return path, else `[proc.out-cond-clobber-unverified]`; (b) the dual and higher-yield — a cond-out register ABSENT from `clobbers` must be provably unwritten on the ¬cc edges (this is what catches the AllocEffect regression). `out_verify.rs` already owns the CFG + return-edge machinery. — **CLOSED 2026-08-04 (B′-0b, delta spec §7.1/§7.2).** Fable ruled (b), error tier: a cond-out register ABSENT from `clobbers` must be PROVABLY PRESERVED on every ¬cc exit. `out_verify::check_cond_out_survives` proves it with `preserves::verify_preserved_on` SCOPED to those exits — the same round-trip / never-written / callee-preserves-oracle proofs, no second dataflow — reading the SAME `flags_after` cc map the production half reads (extracted from out_verify's fused state, which never let its two components read each other). **⊤ DOES NOT OBLIGATE, reversing the overseer's recommendation on measurement.** The corpus could not see the cost (every return in both claim sites is `moveq`-classified, so both polarities give the identical 1 firing); a probe past the corpus showed firing-at-⊤ rejects HONEST contracts at error tier — `clr.w d0` or `move.w #0, d0` as the Z-result convention, any store, and any call on the SUCCESS path all send the flags to ⊤, dragging the cc-success return into the ¬cc set where the register is written by contract. Lens C found the same hole from the other side (a composite cc — `hi`/`ls`/`ge` — that `Flags::refine` cannot refine leaves BOTH returns ⊤). The escape is not free there: `clobbers(rN)` would publish a FALSE claim, the exact polarity §7.2 rejects in the other direction. The two ⊤s differ — a `preserves` bailout means "your claim was checked and the proof gave up"; a ⊤ cc means "I cannot tell whether you made a claim here". Cost: a bounded, pinned FALSE NEGATIVE (`an_all_unclassifiable_body_leaves_the_survives_claim_unchecked`), shrinkable by widening `Flags::after` (row below). Per-file ERROR gate (ClobberAll + the PreserveAll deferral probe, mirroring `check_preserves`), corpus gate under the closure Oracle as final authority (`out_verify_corpus::cond_out_survives_claims_all_prove`, assert-empty, with `survives_claim_sites` pinned so the gate cannot go vacuous). An EXIT is any edge leaving the proc (return or transfer out), not only an `rts`; the transfer's TARGET is deliberately not charged, because a transfer may diverge and nothing marks that yet. The exact AllocEffect hoisted-pop regression this row names now FIRES; `AllocEffect` is the pinned passing witness. — DONE
- [bprime-0 lens C, 2026-08-04] **`contract_type_bound` (corpus_contracts.rs:972-981) DROPS `sig.out` entirely — an indirect-dispatch soundness hole with a DESTRUCTIVE failure mode.** `extern_node` twenty lines above deliberately does `effective = clobbers ∪ out` ("an `out` register is WRITTEN by the callee ... a caller relying on it across the call is wrong and must be charged it"); the contract-type path does not. Live shape: `player_sensors.emp:49` `type SensorProbe = proc (…) clobbers(d3-d5/a1) out(d0, d1, d2)` dispatched via `jsr (a2)` — the bound contributes only `{d3,d4,d5,a1}`, so `d0/d1/d2` never enter `effective` at the indirect site. A proc whose only writer of d1 is that dispatch passes §9; a caller holding d1 across it gets no D1c firing; and **`find_dead_saves` then reports the caller's `movem.l d1,-(sp)` as `[proc.dead-save]` — the D1d worklist actively tells a porter to DELETE a load-bearing save.** Not exhibiting today only because `Player_SensorPair` writes d0-d2 locally. Pre-existing, NOT introduced by B'-0. Fix: union `expand_reglist_regs(t.sig.out)` into the returned `RegEffect.regs`. — **CLOSED (B′-0c item 1).** `contract_type_bound` now unions `expand_reglist_regs(t.sig.out)` (conditional outs included — the conservative read, matching `extern_node`) into the returned `RegEffect`. Three pins in `tests/corpus_contracts.rs`: a bounded `jsr (a2) as SensorProbe` charges the bound's `out(d0)`, the same for `out(a1 if eq)`, and declaring the charged register keeps the dispatch clean. Non-vacuity measured — with the union removed both charge tests FAIL. Corpus-neutral (the `player_sensors` shape stays covered by local writes): the whole-corpus §9/D1b/D1c/§6 firing sets are byte-for-byte unchanged.

- [bprime-0 lens C, 2026-08-04] **`cond_guarded` is register-keyed, so `out(a1, a1 if eq) clobbers(a1)` — a genuine UNCONDITIONAL-result-and-scratch contradiction — is also suppressed.** The exemption is exact in the cc dimension (a second guard on the same register only widens correctly) but not in the cond/uncond dimension: one conditional mention exempts the register wholesale, including its unconditional mention. Unreachable in practice (no author writes a register twice in one `out`). Fix if the verification check above lands: key the exemption on the (register, has-unconditional-mention) pair. — **CLOSED 2026-08-04 (B′-0b).** `ProcDecl::cond_only_out_regs(rf)` is the new exemption key: a register is exempt only when EVERY `out()` mention of it carries an `if cc`. It COUNTS reglist segments covering the register against `if cc` clauses naming it rather than subtracting sets, which also catches a RANGE mention — `out(a0-a2, a1 if eq)` covers a1 twice against one guard, so `clobbers(a1)` still errors there. Both walls pinned in `tests/lower_proc.rs` (`an_unconditional_mention_defeats_the_cond_out_exemption`, `a_range_mention_defeats_the_cond_out_exemption`), non-vacuous against `cond_out_may_overlap_clobbers` which is the same declaration minus the unconditional mention. — DONE

### B′-0b era lens panel (A/B/C), 2026-08-04 — the survives-claim verifier

- [bprime-0b lens B/C, 2026-08-04] **`out_verify::Flags::after` folds only `moveq`, while `branch_const::const_flag_writer` already folds `clr` and `move #imm` — two cc-abstract lattices in one crate, and the weaker one now backs an ERROR gate.** Measured: `clr.w d0` and `move.w #0, d0` set Z exactly as `moveq #0, d0` does and `branch_const` proves both; `Flags::after` sends them to ⊤. Under B′-0b's ruling a ⊤ exit is UNOBLIGATED, so today this is a precision loss (a false negative), not a false positive — widening can only ADD checking, which is why the ⊤ reversal made this safe to defer. NOT taken in B′-0b: `Flags::after` also feeds `verify_out`'s PRODUCTION obligation, where more precision means the obligation is DROPPED at more returns — a false-negative direction on a shipping gate whose `[proc.out-unverified]` residue (30 firings) is a recorded surface. The widening must be its own parcel, measuring the residue delta. Related: two `cc_transparent` allowlists disagree (`out_verify`'s lacks `link`/`unlk`/`Scc`, `branch_const`'s lacks the returns and tails) and two identical 14-arm branch-cond tables exist (`out_verify::simple_branch_cond` vs `flag_check::branch_cond`). The honest end state is one `cc_abstract` module owning transfer + allowlist + table, consumed by out_verify, branch_const and flag_check. — OPEN
- [bprime-0b lens C, 2026-08-04] **A survives claim whose ¬cc exits are all unclassifiable goes UNCHECKED — the ⊤ ruling's named cost.** Pinned by `an_all_unclassifiable_body_leaves_the_survives_claim_unchecked` so it stays visible; the test is expected to FLIP when the row above lands. The bound: every exit ending in the corpus's `moveq #0`/`moveq #1` Z-result convention IS classified, which is why both live claim sites are fully checked. — OPEN (accepted cost, tracked)
- [bprime-0b lens C, 2026-08-04] **A cond-out proc with NO `clobbers(...)` clause escapes the survives gate entirely.** Both tiers skip it (`proc.clobbers.is_some()` / `node.has_clobber_contract`), mirroring `check_clobbers`: §7.1's rule reads clobbers MEMBERSHIP, and an absent clause declares nothing. But §7.1's TEXT says "rN ∉ clobbers ⇒ PRESERVED", and an absent clause is a set rN is not in — so the reading is a porter's call inherited from house convention, not a ruling. It is an escape from an ERROR tier that costs at most one WARN. ASK FABLE: rule it, or add a distinct diagnostic for "conditional out with no clobbers clause". — OPEN (ruling ask)
- [bprime-0b lens C, 2026-08-04] **The corpus tier is a strict-suite assertion, not a compiler diagnostic — state the scope, do not call the whole feature "error tier".** The only `[proc.out-cond-survives-unverifiable]` a build emits is the per-file gate's, and that gate defers everything a call / tail / indirect blocks to the corpus walk, whose firings surface only through `out_verify_corpus::cond_out_survives_claims_all_prove` and `emp_contracts`. Measured classes where per-file is clean but the corpus fires: a relabeling wrapper over a cond-out callee, and `jmp (a0)` on the ¬cc edge. Aeon is genuinely backstopped (the strict suite always runs the gate); a Sigil consumer outside the aeon tree is not. Same shape as `check_preserves`' deferral, so this is the house pattern rather than a new hole — but the packet must not claim more than it delivers. — RECORDED
- [bprime-0b lens C, 2026-08-04] **A truthful TRANSITIVE survives claim cannot be proven — `RegEffect` is edge-blind.** A wrapper `jbsr Inner; rts` over `Inner: out(a1 if eq)` cannot carry its own `out(a1 if eq)` survives claim, because `effective(Inner)` contains a1 (Inner writes it on its own cc edge) so the oracle reads the call as clobbering. Correct-but-imprecise today; the AllocDynamic←Load_Object cascade is the corpus shape that would need it. Fix: an edge-sensitive `RegEffect` (per-cc clobber sets), which is also what an edge-precise D1c wants. — OPEN
- [bprime-0b lens C, 2026-08-04] **No `@noreturn` in the language, so a divergent tail cannot be told from a real tail call.** B′-0b resolves it by NOT charging a transfer's target (an error rail owes the caller nothing), accepting the false negative that a tail to a genuinely-clobbering target is missed on the ¬cc edge. The transitive half stays with the closure's `TAIL_MNEMONICS` edge, which folds an UNCONDITIONAL tail's effect into `effective` — a conditional branch out is not a closure edge either, so that sliver is uncovered by both. `@noreturn` would let the charge return with no false positives. — **@noreturn SHIPPED (noreturn-tail lane, 2026-08-05), but this row stays OPEN — noreturn was NOT the sole missing piece.** The language now HAS the divergence distinction (a `Defer` to a `@noreturn` target is a divergent terminal; the cycle-budget consumer already reads it). Charging the out-cond transitive half in `out_verify::check_cond_out_survives`, though, is a change to the SURVIVES verifier's transfer-target handling — corpus_contracts / out_verify call-target code, which the noreturn lane was scoped OUT of (spec §4/§6). So the follow-up that turns the charge on (crediting a tail to a non-`@noreturn` target on the ¬cc edge, exempting `@noreturn` targets) is a separate out_verify parcel that now has the distinction it was blocked on. The residue lane's invoke-edge fix is the sibling consumer.
- [bprime-0b lens A/B, 2026-08-04] **The corpus header prose restates declarations the compiler now enforces.** `tile_cache.emp`'s `// Clobbers: d3-d4, a1` and `// … d0–d2 preserved.` sat two lines above a declaration that says both; B′-0b converted the second into `preserves(d0-d2)` (verified by §5) and left the prose, because deleting one proc's header lines in a file where ~40 siblings keep theirs trades one inconsistency for another. The sweep — delete every header `Clobbers:`/`Preserves:` line whose declaration exists and is checked — is corpus-wide and separable. — OPEN
- [bprime-0b lens C, 2026-08-04] **Two diagnostic-accuracy nits in `preserves.rs` under a `Sites` scope.** `ever_clobbered` is computed whole-body and unscoped, so it counts writes on the cc edge and weakens the `bailed_reached_exit && clobbered` arm's precision; `bail_reason` is the FIRST bail anywhere in the body, so a hazard on the success edge can supply the message text for a failure-edge firing. Neither changes a verdict. — RECORDED

Corpus firings 93 → **12**, tally line 111 → **30**, byte-neutral ×7 at chain 43. See `notes/2026-08-04-path-mismatch-narrowing.md`. — CLOSED
- [path-mismatch, 2026-08-04] **The 12 surviving `module.path-mismatch` firings are two engine-side classes.** (a) `engine.s4lz` / `engine.zx0` / `engine.compression_vectors` live in files named `s4lz_decompress.emp` / `zx0_decompress.emp` / `debug/generated/vectors.emp` — renaming the FILE is the obvious fix. Not fixed: this parcel changed the rule, not the corpus. — UPDATE (renames-hygiene, 2026-08-05): class (a) is DONE — `engine/compression/{s4lz,zx0}.emp` via `git mv`, and the generated module renamed at its source (`tools/gen_compression_vectors.py` now emits `compression_vectors.emp` and removes a stale `vectors.emp`, since the manifest scan would compile a leftover as a duplicate module). Same shape as the 88 `import.no-names` firings, which were sigil warning about the entry module `build_emp` itself synthesises (fixed in-parcel). The desugar class is retired via the `AssertDesugar` exemption (43/42/43-era counts → 0; obligation pinned at the emission site); the entry-module class keeps its `SourceId` exclusion in `collect_warnings` because `use`-statement diagnostics are not `Instr` items (the field is Instr-only by ruling), with `synthetic_entry_emits_no_code` pinning that the synthesis emits nothing an effect lint could charge.

- [process, 2026-08-04] **Merged packets keep declaring themselves UNMERGED.** A close packet's status line is written by the porter before the merge and nothing in the flow updates it after, so the archive is full of merged work whose own header says "Checkpoint for the overseer's countersign + merge. NOT merged." Today's four were corrected in place (B′-0, B′-0c, warn-tier, B′-1) — the B′-1 one was caught by the PORTER on wake-up, not by the overseer who merged it, which is the tell that nothing systematic is watching. **~15 more from 2026-08-01/02 are still stale** (`conv-*`, `sound-e1`/`e2`, `item7a`, `l5-l8`, `l9`, `onesit-batch`). Cheap structural fix: the merge step rewrites the status line, or packets stop carrying a merge-state claim at all and the campaign log stays the single authority for what landed. The second is probably right — a fact duplicated in two places is a fact that will disagree with itself. — CLOSED (renames-hygiene, 2026-08-05): the second fix is ADOPTED (overseer-ruled). Every close-packet status-header merge-state claim in notes/ — the ~15 stale 2026-08-01/02 set AND the later packets whose claims were still true (a true duplicate rots the same way) — now reads `Merge state lives in the campaign log, not here.` 33 packets swept, listed in `notes/2026-08-05-renames-hygiene.md`. Branch provenance ("built off X"), gate numbers, and time-scoped records ("masters at close were X") stay — those are facts about the work, not claims about master. The standing rule: packets carry NO merge-state claims; the campaign log is the single authority.

### The `sr` lane (2026-08-04) — whose machine-state write is it?

- [sr-lane, 2026-08-04] **A context has no declared PERTURBATION SET, so a per-proc lint cannot charge its acquire/release correctly in general.** `[proc.sr-undeclared]`'s region exemption is gated on the acquire+release ROUND-TRIPPING SR, which is the right answer for `ints_off` and provably safe for a context that never restores — but it is a per-lint hand-rolled rule. The general form is a context declaring what it perturbs NET (`context ints_off { perturbs() }` vs a context whose acquire genuinely leaves a scratch register clobbered), so every machine-state lint reads one declaration instead of re-deriving. Sharpest today for `[proc.clobber-undeclared]`: `z80_stopped`'s acquire writes only an absolute address, so nothing is charged now, but the first context whose acquire touches a data register will charge its consumers for it. — **DEFERRED WITH A NAMED TRIGGER 2026-08-05 (codeitem-author design §4, RULED): demand-parked like Z80 `VALID_CCS`** — today's contexts round-trip SR and clobber nothing, so `perturbs(...)` would be an annotation with zero honest users. The `ItemAuthor::Context { name, phase }` field is deliberately the substrate that makes it cheap later: when a genuinely-clobbering acquire appears, `[proc.clobber-undeclared]` charges `Context`-authored writes against the context's then-new declared perturbation instead of the consumer — one clause plus one lookup, no new walk. **TRIGGER: the first context whose acquire writes a register it does not restore.**
- [sr-lane, 2026-08-04] **`preserves` is not discharged through a declared tail transfer.** `QueueDMA_Critical` / `QueueDMA_Important` `jbra QueueDMA_Deferrable.transfer` and carry that core's SR behaviour exactly, but their own bodies contain no SR write, so `check_preserves_sr` would verify a `preserves(sr)` there VACUOUSLY — a manual-honor claim the honest-contract rule prohibits. So the sibling entry points stay silent about a contract they genuinely keep. Same shape as the warn-tier packet's §9 item 1 (`out()` not discharged by a callee's or fallthrough target's declared `out()`), and `closure.rs` already does this reasoning for `clobbers`. One fix would close both. — **CLOSED 2026-08-06 (68k preserves-through-tail credit, spec `specs/2026-08-06-preserves-through-tail-design.md`).** `check_preserves_sr` now credits an unconditional external tail into a mask-preserving sibling (the per-file mask-preservers oracle + `terminal_external_tail`, divergent tails excluded), so `QueueDMA_Critical`/`Important` declare `preserves(sr.mask)` as a CHECKED fact through the `jbra QueueDMA_Deferrable.transfer` tail (the prose apology at dma_queue.emp:80-92 retired to a present-tense contract fact). Byte-identical ×7. The mask credit and the register credit (row 2088) are the one fix this row predicted.
- [sr-lane, 2026-08-04] **The `sr` contract token conflates the interrupt MASK and CCR, and `QueueDMA_Deferrable` is the corpus's first proc to need them apart.** It declares `out(carry: dropped)` AND `preserves(sr)`; carry ⊂ CCR ⊂ SR, so on its face the signature says both "I hand you the carry" and "I preserve SR". Only the header prose resolves it. The ERROR-tier partition check that exists for exactly this — `[proc.out-preserves-overlap]` — is structurally blind here: it walks `preserves` through `preserves_reg_bit`, which answers `None` for `sr`, and `carry` lives in `out_flags`, never in the out reglist. It is the only one of the seven `preserves(sr)` adopters co-declaring an `out(carry: …)`. The honest surface is a split token (`preserves(sr.mask)` / `clobbers(sr.ccr)`, or `preserves(ints)`), after which the partition check can see the overlap it was written to catch. Found independently by two lens panels. — **CLOSED 2026-08-05 by the sr-split lane.** `sr.mask`/`sr.ccr` are contract-position tokens in all three reglist clauses (`preserves(ints)` rejected: the split names the partition of the machine register, not a synonym); bare `sr` stays legal and means both halves; same-clause co-declaration FOLDS (a reglist is a set union — the `d0, d0-d3` precedent) while cross-clause overlap is diagnosed per HALF. `[proc.out-preserves-overlap]` now sees a flag result (and a cond guard) against any CCR-covering `preserves`/`clobbers` — bare `sr` included, which is what made the old `QueueDMA_Deferrable` spelling fire — and stays silent against `sr.mask`; both facts pinned as tests. Both corpus instances flipped honest: `QueueDMA_Deferrable` = `out(carry: dropped) preserves(sr.mask)`, `Sound_DrainSfxRing` = `clobbers(d0-d1/a0/sr.ccr) preserves(sr.mask)`. Verifier: mask claims ride the existing round-trip slice; an explicit `preserves(sr.ccr)` is REFUSED (`[proc.preserves-unverifiable]`) unless every flag effect sits inside the save/restore bracket (the one shape the static slice can prove). MERGE NOTE: both intermediate windows measured RED (new-sigil+old-corpus fires the overlap ERROR at QueueDMA; old-sigil+new-corpus cannot parse the dot) — the parcel lands TOGETHER. — CLOSED
- [sr-lane, 2026-08-04] **"Compiler-authored instructions charged to the user's proc" now has THREE proposed general fixes and no chosen one.** The generated-code provenance marker (warn-tier row above, for the `assert` desugar and the synthetic entry module), the context perturbation set (row above, for spliced acquire/release), and the region exemption actually built here are three answers to one shape. They should be adjudicated together rather than accreting one point solution per lint: the underlying question is whether a `CodeItem` should carry its AUTHOR, which would retire all three. — **CLOSED 2026-08-05 (codeitem-author parcel; design `2026-08-05-codeitem-author-design.md`, RULED).** `CodeItem::Instr` carries `author: ItemAuthor { User, AssertDesugar, Context{name,phase}, EntrySynth, Splice{template} }`. The invariant that keeps it sound: authorship never exempts, it REDIRECTS — the sr lint's exemption is the typed `Context`/`AssertDesugar` check, with the round-trip proof at the context DEFINITION (once per context, at its declaration span), the desugar balance pinned at the emission site, and the entry synthesis pinned to emit nothing. The three point solutions are retired: the `ContextMark`-range walk in `check_clobbers` (deleted with `region_round_trips_sr` — the recognizer survives, shared by the definition check), the source-line-reading property test (replaced by the typed census gate), and the provenance-marker proposal (subsumed).
- [sr-lane, 2026-08-04] **`check_preserves_sr` is a static-ORDER slice with no path analysis, and `QueueDMA_Deferrable` is now its widest customer.** The proc has three restore-and-return exits; the slice checks only "a save precedes the first SR write and the last SR write is a restore", which a proc with a genuinely missing restore on ONE path would also satisfy. The claim was hand-path-checked at declaration time and the packet records the four paths, but the checker is not what proves it. S2-D7's dataflow half is the real answer. — RECORDED
- [sr-split, 2026-08-05] **Bare `preserves(sr)` claims both halves but only the mask half is machine-checked — and the lens panel CLASSIFIED the seven remaining bare adopters by reading every body.** HONEST both halves (correctly bare): `Sound_PostByte` (whole body inside the bracket), `Sound_Init` (save first, restore last), `BG_Init` (only CC-inert ops outside the pair). **CCR HALF FALSE — these four should flip to `preserves(sr.mask)`:** `VSync_Wait` (vblank.emp:273 — `tst.b`/`beq`/`move` flag traffic AFTER the restore; its own header tells a mask-only story), `Sound_PlayMusic` (sound_api.emp:190 — the final bracket's save is mid-proc, so the release restores mid-proc CCR, not entry), `Parallax_Update` (parallax.emp:345 — flag traffic after its bracket, bare `rts`), `GameState_OJZScroll_Init` (ojz_scroll_test.emp:69 — `jbsr` traffic after its bracket). The last two were given bare `preserves(sr)` BY the sr-contracts panel's B3 fix (true for the mask, over-claiming CCR), and `irq.emp` PRESCRIBES bare `sr` for bracket adopters — so the four-flip is also a convention amendment (bracket adopters whose post-bracket code writes flags claim `sr.mask`), which is an overseer/ruling call, not a porter's; deliberately not taken in the sr-split parcel (scope was the two ERROR-firing instances). Mechanical enforcement path exists in-tree: `ccr_bracket_refusal` run ADVISORY (warn tier) over bare-`sr` adopters would name all four with zero new analysis — a sharper interim than waiting for S2-D7. When S2-D7's dataflow half lands, decide whether bare `sr` upgrades to a checked both-halves claim or stays the historical bar. — **CLOSED 2026-08-05 by the bare-sr-flip lane (overseer-ruled).** All four flipped to `preserves(sr.mask)` (one-token edits + present-tense header truth-fixes on VSync_Wait/Sound_PlayMusic; each body re-verified against the panel's classification before flipping — all four confirmed CCR-false), and `irq.emp`'s bracket-adopter prescription amended: an adopter declares the claim its WHOLE BODY earns — `sr.mask` when post-bracket code writes flags, bare `sr` only when every CCR effect sits inside the pair. The mask claims stay machine-checked (`check_preserves_sr` runs for `sr.mask` exactly as for bare `sr` — same round-trip slice, non-vacuous in all four since each body splices the save/restore; corpus builds clean in every shape). The three honest bare adopters stay bare. **The ADVISORY DETECTOR WAS NOT ENABLED — the "zero new analysis" claim is false in DEBUG shapes:** `ccr_bracket_refusal` classifies `jmp` as `CcrEffect::Leaves` (unconditional refusal), and the `raise_error`/`assert` desugar ends in `jmp (pages).l` — so the walk false-positives on `Sound_Init` (honest adopter, DEBUG watchdog `raise_error` inside its bracket) and on every assert-carrying bare-`sr` proc. Enabling takes: (1) a noreturn/diverging-tail model in the walk (the desugar's `jmp` never returns to the caller, so its flags never reach one); (2) a new warn id + DEBUG-shape warn-tier baseline row; (3) a fixture audit (lower_proc.rs unbalanced-SR fixtures would double-fire, diag_assert_vector.rs's `preserves(sr)` vector proc, diag_desugar parity fixtures); (4) a detector test. Left for the parcel that builds the noreturn model (or S2-D7's dataflow half, which subsumes it). The S2-D7 upgrade-or-historical-bar decision above stays live at that milestone. — **ADVISORY ENABLED 2026-08-05 by the noreturn-tail lane.** `[proc.ccr-advisory]` (warn tier, new id) now runs `ccr_bracket_refusal` over bare-`sr` adopters, gated on the mask round-trip PASSING (so it is CCR-SPECIFIC — a proc already failing the round-trip is not double-nagged; that gate is the fixture-audit answer to the lower_proc unbalanced-SR fixtures, which the packet records). All four enabling items shipped: (i) divergence awareness — `ItemAuthor::AssertDesugar` rail items are SKIPPED (the raise/assert `move.w sr, -(sp)` frame push no longer reads as a nested save, the `jmp (pages)` no longer as a `Leaves`) AND a User transfer to a `@noreturn` target is a divergent terminal; (ii) local-label awareness — a `jbra .local` is intra-proc flow via the shared `Cfg`, not a leave; (iii) the new warn id + DEBUG-shape baselines (MEASURED: ZERO firings in every shape — plain/debug/demo/demodbg/config_a/config_b/lean all UNCHANGED from baseline, confirming the bare-sr flip was complete and no honest adopter regressed); (iv) fixture audit + detector tests (4 in lower_proc.rs incl. the DEBUG-rail-inside-bracket case; diag_desugar's `golden` transliterations flipped to `preserves(sr.mask)` — the hand-written frame-sim save is `User`-authored and unprovable by a linear walk, while their `construct` twins keep bare `sr` since the desugar's traffic is emission-site-proven). The S2-D7 upgrade-or-historical-bar decision stays live.
- [sr-split, 2026-08-05] **The `sr.ccr` slice shares the static-order blindness of its mask sibling.** `ccr_bracket_refusal` walks items in order, so a conditional branch AROUND the restore (save → flags-work → `beq .past_restore`) verifies while one path returns unclean — the same one-path hole the row above records for `check_preserves_sr`, in the same S2-D7 bucket. It is strictly sharper than the mask slice in the directions that were free (refuses a return inside the bracket, a call or tail anywhere unbracketed, nested saves), and it REFUSES rather than trusts everything else, so the hole is only reachable through a branch the walk cannot see. Zero corpus adopters today; the refusal-first stance is the guard until dataflow lands. — RECORDED
- [sr-split, 2026-08-05] **CCR-only destinations are invisible to `[proc.sr-undeclared]`.** The lint's trigger is an `Sr`-destination operand, so `andi.b #$FE, ccr` (destroys carry) in a proc with NO SR-family declaration is silent — the corpus's ccr-destination sites (`rings.emp`, `buffers.emp`, `dma_queue.emp`) are all in procs whose contracts address CCR, but nothing enforces that. The split created the vocabulary the write should be charged against (`clobbers(sr.ccr)`/`out(carry: …)`); extending the lint to `Ccr` destinations is small but moves warn-tier counts, so it is a deliberate baseline edit for a parcel that measures it — not taken in the sr-split parcel (found by its Lens C). — OPEN
- [sr-split, 2026-08-05] **The Z80 flag surface has no partition check at all.** `check_out`'s Z80 arm returns before the SR-half logic (SR is 68k machine state — correct), but a Z80 `out(carry: …)` against `preserves(af)` is the same returned-vs-untouched contradiction with nobody looking. Zero corpus instances; recorded so the first one is a known shape, not a discovery. — RECORDED
- [sr-split, 2026-08-05] **`extern proc` signatures and proc contract types accept the SR family but no consumer reads it there.** `ProcSig` parses `preserves(sr.mask)` etc. through the shared reglist grammar, and the closure/conformance machinery drops the SR family exactly as it always dropped bare `sr` (register-file only). Pre-existing shape inherited by the halves, recorded so the first `extern proc` carrying an SR claim knows it is prose until a consumer exists. — RECORDED
- [bare-sr-flip lens C, 2026-08-05 · reworded residue-b9 2026-08-05] **`preserves`-through-tail-transfer credit for the `QueueDMA_Critical`/`Important` siblings (the RedrawPlanes half is CLOSED).** The `Section_RedrawPlanes` half of the original row is DONE: residue-b9 flipped `clobbers(…, sr)` → `clobbers(d0-d4/d6/a0-a6/sr.ccr) preserves(sr.mask)` (section.emp), checker-verified by the same slices that prove QueueDMA_Deferrable and Sound_DrainSfxRing, byte-identical across the ×7 bar. What STAYS OPEN is the sibling-credit demand documented at dma_queue.emp:80: `QueueDMA_Critical`/`Important` reach the shared `QueueDMA_Deferrable.transfer` core through a tail transfer, and cannot declare the `preserves(sr.mask)` their body-via-tail earns because the 68k PRESERVES closure does not credit a tail transfer to a preserving callee. The Z80 sibling already does the honest thing — `z80_preserves` credits a callee's declared preservation across its external-tail / `Edge::Defer` arm (the `callee_preserves` oracle applied at z80_preserves.rs's tail/Defer sites) — so the design precedent for the 68k credit exists; porting it would let both DMA siblings and this whole tail-into-preserver class state the true thing. — OPEN (sibling-credit half only) **Precedent citations (noreturn-tail lane, 2026-08-05):** the Z80 credit is the `CalleePreserves` oracle consulted at `z80_preserves.rs`'s tail/`Edge::Defer` arms (:60/:305/:337); the 68k `preserves.rs` `BalanceObserver`/`PreserveObserver` Defer arm has no such consult (`ExitKind::Defer` → not credited) — that consult is the 68k mirror this row asks for, its own parcel. — **CLOSED 2026-08-06 (68k preserves-through-tail credit).** The `PreserveObserver` `Edge::Defer` exit under `ReturnScope::AllReturns` is now an obligation site charged through the `CallPolicy` oracle (`apply_callee_effect` at the tail), the 68k mirror of the Z80 credit — closing the tail-only vacuity hole and granting the sibling-credit in one motion. `@noreturn`/`AssertDesugar` tails diverge (no obligation); `falls_into` threads the empty-body + FallOff arms. ADOPTION (all byte-neutral ×7): register credit — `Sound_PlayRing (+preserves(a0))` through its two `jbra Sound_PlaySFX` tails (`d1` is preserved by the identical argument — the body touches only d0 and Sound_PlaySFX preserves d1/a0 — but LEFT OFF the contract until a caller needs it); sr.mask credit (row 2079) — `QueueDMA_Critical`/`Important` + `Sound_Ping`/`PlaySample`/`StopMusic`/`SetTempo`/`FadeOut`/`FadeIn` (`+preserves(sr.mask)` each, tailing into `Sound_PostByte`). CORPUS CENSUS restated under BOTH preserver definitions: **DECLARED-preserver census** (a tail into a callee whose DECLARED contract preserves the reg — Sound_PostByte `preserves(sr)`, Sound_PlaySFX `preserves(d1/a0)`, QueueDMA_Deferrable `preserves(sr.mask)`): **9 sites, ALL adopted** (the named list); no refusals, no width findings (row 2142 untouched). **CLOSURE-preserver census** (a tail into a callee that does not CLOBBER the reg per the transitive closure, though it declares no `preserves` — the object idiom `jbra Draw_Sprite` / `jbra DeleteObject`, where a0 survives by non-clobber): the Draw_Sprite/DeleteObject tail class = ~18 terminal sites across ~14 games/ object+player Main routines (test_churn/test_solid/test_stress_emitter/demo_box/sonic/test_particle/test_player/path_swap/test_parent/test_emitter/test_static/test_enemy/test_animated + player_common), of which `TestChurnObj_Main` (test_churn.emp:76) ALREADY declares `preserves(a0)` — the precedent. Adoption of the rest DEFERRED as a test-object contract-hygiene pass OUTSIDE spec §4's engine/sound scope; `TestChildPart_Main` (test_parent.emp) is a representative (its `preserves(a0)` proves, but its second `jbra Draw_Sprite` tail clobbers d2, so d2 refuses — the general shape of the class). — **CLOSURE-PRESERVER CENSUS CLOSED 2026-08-06 (lane hyg, contract text only).** The deferred test-object contract-hygiene pass ran: `preserves(a0)` adopted on all 15 remaining tail-class Mains and every one VERIFIES through the 68k closure credit (zero `[proc.preserves-unverifiable]` refusals) — DemoBox_Main, Player_DebugMove, Sonic_LoadArt (through `jbsr Perform_DPLC` + `jbra Draw_Sprite`), TestEnemy_Main, TestParticle_Main, TestEmitter_Main, TestStressEmitter_Main, TestStatic_Main, TestPlayer_Main, TestPlayer_Debug, TestAnimated_Main, PathSwap_Main, TestSolid_Main, plus test_parent's TestChildPart_Main and TestParent_Main. With TestChurnObj_Main's precedent the class is now 16/16 declaring `preserves(a0)`. Only a0 was claimed — d2 was NOT attempted on TestChildPart_Main (the row's caveat: its second `jbra Draw_Sprite` tail clobbers d2), so the adoption stayed inside what the closure proves. Byte-neutral ×7 (contract text is comptime, zero ROM bytes); warn-tier id sets unmoved.
- [preserves-through-tail, 2026-08-06] **The sr.mask tail credit is TERMINAL-only; the register credit charges EVERY Defer — an asymmetry with one true-but-unproven witness.** The register-preserves walk makes every `Edge::Defer` exit an obligation (all paths), but the sr.mask credit (`check_preserves_sr`/`terminal_external_tail`) only inspects the body's LAST instruction, so a MID-body external tail that leaves with a foreign mask is invisible to the mask claim.
